```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# SessionEnd hook: export the session transcript (plus any subagent transcripts)
# to docs/agent-log/<session-id>.md and commit that file.
#
# Reads the hook payload on stdin:
#   {"session_id": "...", "transcript_path": "...", "cwd": "...", "reason": "..."}
# Can also be run by hand with the same JSON on stdin.
#
# Dependencies: bash, jq, git.
set -euo pipefail

here=$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)
filter="$here/transcript-to-md.jq"

input=$(cat)
sid=$(jq -r '.session_id // empty' <<<"$input")
tp=$(jq -r '.transcript_path // empty' <<<"$input")
reason=$(jq -r '.reason // "manual"' <<<"$input")
cwd=$(jq -r '.cwd // empty' <<<"$input")
root=$(git -C "${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-${cwd:-$here}}" rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>/dev/null) || root=$(git -C "$here" rev-parse --show-toplevel)

if [[ -z "$sid" || -z "$tp" ]]; then
  echo "export-session: missing session_id or transcript_path in hook input" >&2
  exit 1
fi
if [[ ! -s "$tp" ]]; then
  echo "export-session: transcript $tp missing or empty, nothing to export" >&2
  exit 0
fi

outdir="$root/docs/agent-log"
out="$outdir/$sid.md"
mkdir -p "$outdir"
trap 'rm -f "$out.tmp"' EXIT

render() { # render <heading-prefix> ; records on stdin
  jq -rs --arg h "$1" -f "$filter"
}

meta() { # meta <jq expr> over the main transcript
  jq -rs "$1" "$tp"
}

{
  echo "# Agent session $sid"
  echo
  echo "| | |"
  echo "|---|---|"
  echo "| Project | \`$(meta '[.[] | .cwd // empty] | first // ""')\` |"
  echo "| Branch | \`$(meta '[.[] | .gitBranch // empty] | first // ""')\` |"
  echo "| Claude Code | $(meta '[.[] | .version // empty] | first // ""') |"
  echo "| Started | $(meta '[.[] | .timestamp // empty] | min // ""') |"
  echo "| Last record | $(meta '[.[] | .timestamp // empty] | max // ""') |"
  echo "| Exported | $(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ) (reason: $reason) |"
  echo "| Source | \`$tp\` |"
  echo
  jq -c 'select(.isSidechain != true)' "$tp" | render "##"

  # Subagent transcripts, newer layout: <transcript-dir>/<session-id>/subagents/agent-*.jsonl
  subdir="${tp%.jsonl}/subagents"
  if [[ -d "$subdir" ]]; then
    for f in "$subdir"/*.jsonl; do
      [[ -s "$f" ]] || continue
      name=$(basename "$f" .jsonl)
      meta="${f%.jsonl}.meta.json"
      desc=""; atype=""
      if [[ -s "$meta" ]]; then
        desc=$(jq -r '.description // empty' "$meta")
        atype=$(jq -r '.agentType // empty' "$meta")
      fi
      echo; echo "---"; echo
      echo "## Subagent \`$name\`${desc:+: $desc}"
      echo
      echo "Type: \`${atype:-unknown}\` · Source: \`$f\`"
      echo
      jq -c '.' "$f" | render "###"
    done
  fi

  # Subagent transcripts, older layout: sidechain records inside the main file.
  for agent in $(jq -r 'select(.isSidechain == true) | .agentId // "sidechain"' "$tp" | sort -u); do
    echo; echo "---"; echo
    echo "## Subagent \`$agent\` (inline sidechain)"
    echo
    jq -c --arg a "$agent" 'select(.isSidechain == true and ((.agentId // "sidechain") == $a))' "$tp" | render "###"
  done
} | sed -e "s|${HOME}|~|g" > "$out.tmp"   # redact the home directory path
mv "$out.tmp" "$out"

echo "export-session: wrote ${out#$root/}" >&2

# Commit only this file, leaving any other staged or unstaged work untouched.
git -C "$root" add -- "$out"
if git -C "$root" diff --cached --quiet -- "$out"; then
  echo "export-session: no changes to commit" >&2
  exit 0
fi
if git -C "$root" commit -q --only -m "docs(agent-log): export session $sid" -- "$out"; then
  echo "export-session: committed $(git -C "$root" rev-parse --short HEAD)" >&2
else
  echo "export-session: commit failed, export left in working tree" >&2
fi
b630f25 feat: label subagent sections in agent-log export with their description
434d7e1 fix: redact home directory path in agent-log export
0a016d3 fix: agent-log export aborts on render errors; avoid jq reserved word
```

The hook is fixed and committed as `39be0e0` on branch `fix/export-session-bad-lines`, in the worktree `../parcel-rates-hook-fix`. I haven't merged it.

**Cause:** line 1301 of the transcript for session `05ded788…` is corrupt. Claude Code cut one record off partway through writing it (it ends at `"stop_sequence":n`) and then wrote the next record onto the same line. The hook reads the transcript with `jq` in strict mode, so that one line made every step fail. The six identical errors came from the six header lookups (project, branch, version, etc.). The rest of the export then failed on the same line.

**Fix** (`.claude/hooks/export-session.sh`):
- A new `clean()` step keeps only the lines that parse as JSON and prints a warning for each one it skips: `export-session: skipped N unparseable line(s) in <file>`.
- The main transcript is cleaned once into a temp file, and every later step reads from that file. Subagent transcripts go through the same cleaning.

**Tested:** I ran the fixed hook on that same transcript, writing to a scratch repo. It skipped 1 line, exported the rest and committed normally.

**What's lost:** the skipped line held two records, the cut-off one and the one written after it, so both are missing from that export. The other 3,301 lines are intact.

Two things to decide:
1. Should I merge the branch into `main`?
2. That session never got exported. Once the fix is merged, I can run the hook by hand to export and commit it to `docs/agent-log/`.

```
README.md
# Render a Claude Code JSONL transcript as readable Markdown.
#
# Usage: jq -rs --arg h "##" -f transcript-to-md.jq transcript.jsonl
#   $h  heading prefix for each turn ("##" for the main session, "###" for subagents)
#
# One record per JSONL line. Only user / assistant / non-meta system records are
# rendered; bookkeeping records (attachment, mode, snapshots, ...) are skipped.
# Assistant records arrive one content block per line, so consecutive records
# from the same role are merged under a single heading. Tool results (user
# records that only carry tool_result blocks) are kept inside the assistant turn.

def fence(s):
  ([s | scan("`+") | length] | max // 0) as $m
  | ("`" * ([3, $m + 1] | max)) as $f
  | "\($f)\n\(s)\n\($f)";

def escape_tags:
  gsub("<(?<t>/?system-reminder)>"; "&lt;\(.t)&gt;");

def details(summary; body):
  "<details>\n<summary>\(summary)</summary>\n\n\(body)\n\n</details>";

def ts:
  (.timestamp // "") | sub("\\.[0-9]+Z$"; "Z");

def inline_ok:
  (test("[`\n]") | not) and (length <= 120);

def render_input:
  if type == "object" then
    [ to_entries[]
      | .key as $k | .value as $v
      | if ($v | type) == "string" then
          if ($v | inline_ok) then "- `\($k)`: `\($v)`"
          else "- `\($k)`:\n\n\(fence($v))\n" end
        else "- `\($k)`: `\($v | tojson)`" end
    ] | join("\n")
  else fence(tojson) end;

def result_text:
  .content
  | if type == "string" then .
    elif type == "array" then
      [ .[] | if .type == "text" then .text
              elif .type == "image" then "[image]"
              else tojson end ] | join("\n")
    else (. // "" | tostring) end;

def block:
  if .type == "text" then (.text | escape_tags)
  elif .type == "thinking" then
    if ((.thinking // "") | length) == 0 then empty
    else details("Thinking"; .thinking | escape_tags) end
  elif .type == "tool_use" then
    "**Tool call:** `\(.name)` <sub>\(.id)</sub>\n\n\(.input | render_input)"
  elif .type == "tool_result" then
    ("**Tool result" + (if .is_error == true then " (error)" else "" end)
      + "** <sub>\(.tool_use_id)</sub>") as $s
    | details($s; fence(result_text | escape_tags))
  elif .type == "image" then "[image]"
  else fence(tojson) end;

def is_tool_result_only:
  .type == "user"
  and (.message.content | type) == "array"
  and (.message.content | length) > 0
  and all(.message.content[]; .type == "tool_result");

def role:
  if is_tool_result_only then "assistant" else .type end;

def role_label:
  if .type == "user" then "User"
  elif .type == "assistant" then "Assistant"
  else "System" end;

def heading:
  "\($h) \(role_label) <sub>\(ts)</sub>";

def body:
```

````
def body:
  if .type == "system" then
    "> **system\(if .subtype then " (\(.subtype))" else "" end):** \((.content // "") | tostring | escape_tags)"
  elif (.message.content | type) == "string" then
    (.message.content | escape_tags)
  else
    [ .message.content[] | block ] | join("\n\n")
  end;

def renderable:
  ((.type == "user" or .type == "assistant") and .message != null)
  or (.type == "system" and .isMeta != true and .content != null);

map(select(renderable))
| reduce .[] as $r ({prev: null, out: []};
    ($r | role) as $role
    | .out += (if $role != .prev then [($r | heading)] else [] end) + ([($r | body)] | map(select(length > 0)))
    | .prev = $role)
| .out
| join("\n\n")
````

```
def user_text(turn: Turn) -> str:
    return html.unescape(REMINDER.sub("", "\n".join(s.text for s in turn.segments if s.kind in ("text", "system")))).strip()


def tag(text: str, name: str) -> str:
    m = re.search(rf"<{name}>(.*?)</{name}>", text, re.S)
    return m.group(1).strip() if m else ""


def user_kind(turn: Turn) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """(kind, label) of a user turn. The harness speaks as the user for some messages: a
    background task finishing, a subagent handing back its report, a local slash command."""
    text = user_text(turn)
    if text.startswith("<task-notification>"):
        status = tag(text, "status")
        return "event", f"Background task {status}: {tag(text, 'summary')}" if status else "Background task"
    if text.startswith("Another Claude session sent a message"):
        sender = re.search(r'<agent-message from="([^"]+)"', text)
        return "event", "Report from a subagent" + (f" ({sender.group(1)[:8]})" if sender else "")
    if "<local-command-caveat>" in text or text.startswith("<command-name>"):
        name, args = tag(text, "command-name"), tag(text, "command-args")
        return "event", f"Local command {name} {args}".strip()
    if text == INTERRUPTED:
        return "event", "Interrupted by the user"
    if not re.sub(r"\[Image[^\]]*\]|\[image\]", "", text).strip():
        return "event", "An image (a screenshot the session read)" if text else "An empty message"
    return "prompt", ""


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- parsing
def parse_session(path: Path) -> Session:
    lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").replace("\x00", "").split("\n")
    meta: dict[str, str] = {}
    turns: list[Turn] = []
    subagents: list[Subagent] = []
    current: Turn | None = None
    body: list[str] = []
    fence = FenceTracker()
    target = turns

    def close() -> None:
        nonlocal current
        if current is not None:
            current.segments = parse_segments(body)
            # The hook merges records of the same role; join a turn split by a subagent boundary.
            target.append(current)
        current = None
        body.clear()

    for idx, line in enumerate(lines):
        if current is None and not turns and not subagents:
            row = META_ROW.match(line)
            if row and row.group(1).strip() not in ("", "---"):
                meta[row.group(1).strip()] = row.group(2).strip().strip("`")
        if fence.open is None:
            turn = TURN.match(line)
            sub = SUBAGENT.match(line)
            if turn:
                close()
                current = Turn(turn.group(2), turn.group(3))
                continue
            if sub:
                close()
                agent_type = ""
                for look in lines[idx + 1 : idx + 4]:
                    t = SUBAGENT_TYPE.match(look)
                    if t:
                        agent_type = t.group(1)
                subagents.append(Subagent(sub.group(1), (sub.group(2) or "").strip(), agent_type))
                target = subagents[-1].turns
                continue
            if current is not None and line.strip() == "---" and idx + 2 < len(lines) and SUBAGENT.match(lines[idx + 2]):
                continue
        if current is not None:
            fence.step(line)
            body.append(line)
    close()
    for group in [turns] + [sub.turns for sub in subagents]:
        attach_across_turns(group)
    return Session(path.stem, meta, turns, subagents)
```

```
# /// script
# requires-python = ">=3.10"
# dependencies = ["markdown-it-py>=3"]
# ///
"""Render the exported agent sessions (docs/agent-log/*.md) as local HTML pages.

A local viewer for people with access to the repository; nothing is published. The
SessionEnd hook (.claude/hooks/export-session.sh) writes one Markdown file per session;
this script reads those files back into turns (prompts, answers, thinking, tool calls with
their results, subagents) and writes a browsable site:

    uv run tools/agent_log_html.py            # writes build/agent-log/, prints the index path
    uv run tools/agent_log_html.py --open     # and opens it in the browser

The output folder is git-ignored. Sessions without a prompt are skipped.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import html
import json
import re
import sys
import webbrowser
from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from markdown_it import MarkdownIt

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from agent_log_stats import BUILTINS, GROUPS, Stats, session_stats  # noqa: E402

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
LOG_DIR = ROOT / "docs" / "agent-log"
OUT_DIR = ROOT / "build" / "agent-log"

MD = MarkdownIt("commonmark", {"html": False, "linkify": False}).enable("table").enable("strikethrough")

TURN = re.compile(r"^(#{2,3}) (User|Assistant|System) <sub>(.*?)</sub>\s*$")
SUBAGENT = re.compile(r"^## Subagent `([^`]+)`(?:: (.*))?\s*$")
SUBAGENT_TYPE = re.compile(r"^Type: `([^`]*)`")
FENCE = re.compile(r"^ {0,3}(`{3,}|~{3,})(.*)$")
TOOL_CALL = re.compile(r"^\*\*Tool call:\*\* `([^`]+)` <sub>([^<]+)</sub>\s*$")
RESULT_SUMMARY = re.compile(r"^<summary>\*\*Tool result( \(error\))?\*\* <sub>([^<]+)</sub></summary>\s*$")
REMINDER = re.compile(r"&lt;system-reminder&gt;(.*?)&lt;/system-reminder&gt;", re.S)
META_ROW = re.compile(r"^\| (.+?) \| (.*) \|\s*$")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- model
@dataclass
class Segment:
    kind: str  # text | thinking | tool | result | system
    text: str = ""
    name: str = ""
    tool_id: str = ""
    inputs: list[tuple[str, str, bool]] = field(default_factory=list)  # (key, value, multiline)
    error: bool = False
    result: "Segment | None" = None


@dataclass
class Turn:
    role: str  # User | Assistant | System
    time: str
    segments: list[Segment] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class Subagent:
    name: str
    description: str
    agent_type: str
    turns: list[Turn] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class Session:
    sid: str
    meta: dict[str, str]
    turns: list[Turn]
    subagents: list[Subagent]

    @property
    def prompts(self) -> list[Turn]:
        """The owner's own prompts; notifications, subagent reports and local commands excluded."""
        return [t for t in self.turns if t.role == "User" and user_kind(t)[0] == "prompt"]

    @property
    def tool_calls(self) -> int:
        all_turns = self.turns + [t for s in self.subagents for t in s.turns]
        return sum(1 for t in all_turns for s in t.segments if s.kind == "tool")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- user turn kinds
INTERRUPTED = "[Request interrupted by user]"

def parse_segments(lines: list[str]) -> list[Segment]:
    segments: list[Segment] = []
    fence = FenceTracker()
    buf: list[str] = []
    i = 0

    def flush_text() -> None:
        text = "\n".join(buf).strip()
        if text:
            if text.startswith("> **system"):
                segments.append(Segment("system", text=text.removeprefix("> ")))
            else:
                segments.append(Segment("text", text=text))
        buf.clear()

    while i < len(lines):
        line = lines[i]
        if fence.open is None:
            call = TOOL_CALL.match(line)
            nxt = lines[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(lines) else ""
            if call:
                flush_text()
                j = i + 1
                body: list[str] = []
                inner = FenceTracker()
                while j < len(lines):
                    if inner.open is None and (TOOL_CALL.match(lines[j]) or lines[j].strip() == "<details>"):
                        break
                    inner.step(lines[j])
                    body.append(lines[j])
                    j += 1
                segments.append(Segment("tool", name=call.group(1), tool_id=call.group(2).strip(), inputs=parse_inputs(body)))
                i = j
                continue
            if line.strip() == "<details>" and (nxt.startswith("<summary>Thinking</summary>") or RESULT_SUMMARY.match(nxt)):
                flush_text()
                result = RESULT_SUMMARY.match(nxt)
                j = i + 2
                body = []
                inner = FenceTracker()
                while j < len(lines) and not (inner.open is None and lines[j].strip() == "</details>"):
                    inner.step(lines[j])
                    body.append(lines[j])
                    j += 1
                text = "\n".join(body).strip()
                if result:
                    m = re.match(r"^(`{3,})\n(.*)\n\1$", text, re.S)
                    segments.append(Segment("result", text=m.group(2) if m else text, tool_id=result.group(2).strip(),
                                            error=bool(result.group(1))))
                else:
                    segments.append(Segment("thinking", text=text))
                i = j + 1
                continue
        fence.step(line)
        buf.append(line)
        i += 1
    flush_text()

    # Attach each result to its call; a result without a call stays on its own.
    calls = {s.tool_id: s for s in segments if s.kind == "tool"}
    kept = []
    for s in segments:
        if s.kind == "result" and s.tool_id in calls and calls[s.tool_id].result is None:
            calls[s.tool_id].result = s
        else:
            kept.append(s)
    return kept


        attach_across_turns(group)
    return Session(path.stem, meta, turns, subagents)


def attach_across_turns(turns: list[Turn]) -> None:
    """A result the hook wrote under the next heading still belongs to its call."""
    waiting = {s.tool_id: s for t in turns for s in t.segments if s.kind == "tool" and s.result is None}
    for t in turns:
        kept = []
        for s in t.segments:
            if s.kind == "result" and s.tool_id in waiting:
                waiting.pop(s.tool_id).result = s
            else:
                kept.append(s)
        t.segments = kept


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- rendering
```

```
| | |

Looking first.

def test_turns_ignore_headings_inside_fences(tmp_path):
    s = session(tmp_path)
    assert [t.role for t in s.turns] == ["User", "Assistant", "User", "Assistant", "User"]
    assert s.meta["Branch"] == "main" and s.meta["Started"].startswith("2026-10-22")


def test_tool_calls_get_their_results_also_across_turns(tmp_path):
    s = session(tmp_path)
    bash, read = [g for t in s.turns for g in t.segments if g.kind == "tool"]
    assert bash.name == "Bash" and dict((k, v) for k, v, _ in bash.inputs)["description"] == "Read the notes"
    assert "## User <sub>not a turn" in dict((k, v) for k, v, _ in bash.inputs)["command"]
    assert bash.result is not None and "## Assistant" in bash.result.text and not bash.result.error
    assert read.result is not None and read.result.error and read.result.text == "no such file"
    assert not [g for t in s.turns for g in t.segments if g.kind == "result"]
    assert a.descriptor(bash) == "Read the notes"


def test_prompts_exclude_harness_messages(tmp_path):
    s = session(tmp_path)
    kinds = [a.user_kind(t) for t in s.turns if t.role == "User"]
    assert [k for k, _ in kinds] == ["prompt", "event", "prompt"]
    assert kinds[1][1] == 'Background task completed: Background command "x" completed'
    assert len(s.prompts) == 2 and a.first_line(s.prompts[1]) == "and also this"


def test_subagents_and_rendering(tmp_path):
    s = session(tmp_path)
    assert [(x.name, x.description, x.agent_type, len(x.turns)) for x in s.subagents] == [
        ("agent-1", "Survey the sources", "general-purpose", 1)
    ]
    page = a.render_session(s)
    assert 'class="ctx"' in page and "secret context" in page  # injected context, collapsed
    assert "after interrupting" in page and 'class="event"' in page
    assert page.count('class="tool') >= 2 and "Thinking" in page


def test_build_writes_the_site(tmp_path):
    logs = tmp_path / "logs"
    logs.mkdir()
    (logs / "abc12345-0000.md").write_text(LOG, encoding="utf-8")
    (logs / "empty-0000.md").write_text("# Agent session empty\n\n| | |\n|---|---|\n", encoding="utf-8")
    index, sessions = a.build(logs, tmp_path / "out")
    assert [s.sid for s in sessions] == ["abc12345-0000"]  # the empty session is skipped
    assert (tmp_path / "out" / "sessions" / "abc12345-0000.html").exists() and "Build the thing" in index.read_text()
```

```
COMMAND_END = re.compile(r"</(?:local-command-caveat|command-name|command-message|command-args|local-command-stdout|local-command-stderr)>")


def split_command_prompts(turns: list[Turn]) -> list[Turn]:
    """The hook merges consecutive user records, so a prompt typed right after a local slash
    command shares its heading. Give the text after the command's output a turn of its own."""
    out: list[Turn] = []
    for t in turns:
        texts = [s for s in t.segments if s.kind == "text"]
        ends = [m.end() for m in COMMAND_END.finditer(texts[-1].text)] if t.role == "User" and texts else []
        rest = texts[-1].text[ends[-1]:].strip() if ends else ""
        if not rest:
            out.append(t)
            continue
        last = texts[-1]
        command = Segment("text", text=last.text[: ends[-1]])
        out.append(Turn(t.role, t.time, [command if s is last else s for s in t.segments]))
        out.append(Turn(t.role, t.time, [Segment("text", text=rest)]))
    return out
''')
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
```

Your first prompt was there, but the viewer had it labelled as part of a `/model` command.

**Why:** you ran `/model` before typing "continue working on MVP issues 28, 29…". The export hook groups back-to-back user messages under a single "User" heading, so the `/model` output and your prompt became one turn. The viewer saw the local-command markup at the start of that turn and labelled all of it "Local command /model". Your prompt was hidden inside that event and left out of the prompt list.

**Fix** (`tools/agent_log_html.py`, committed as `c1fb1d4` on branch `fix/viewer-command-then-prompt`, in worktree `../parcel-rates-viewer-fix`):
- When a user turn contains local-command markup followed by more text, the viewer now splits that text into its own prompt turn.
- I added a test for this case. All 25 tests pass.
- After rebuilding, c1ff8718 shows 3 prompts instead of 2, and Prompt 1 is "continue working on MVP issues 28, 29, 30, 31…".

The page now starts with the "agents-md" system notice, then the `/model` event, then Prompt 1. That's the real order of events.

I fixed this in the viewer rather than the export hook, so the session logs already committed display correctly without re-exporting them. The other two sessions with no prompts, 6ed76c95 and 841a71da, contain no user messages at all, so the viewer skips them as designed.

```
"""


def build(log_dir: Path, out_dir: Path) -> tuple[Path, list[Session]]:
    sessions = [parse_session(p) for p in sorted(log_dir.glob("*.md")) if p.name != "README.md"]
    sessions = [s for s in sessions if s.prompts]
    sessions.sort(key=lambda s: s.meta.get("Started", ""), reverse=True)
    (out_dir / "sessions").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (out_dir / "assets").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    (out_dir / "assets" / "app.js").write_text(JS.strip() + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
```

````
Exit code 1
# Parcel rates: USPS, UPS and FedEx list prices for a small online shop

[![CI](https://github.com/aivuk/parcel-rates/actions/workflows/ci.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/aivuk/parcel-rates/actions/workflows/ci.yml)

A dashboard that prices a shop's own parcels at the published prices of the
three US parcel carriers, for every year from 2023 to 2026, and shows which
carrier is cheapest, where the breakeven points are, how much of the bill is
surcharges, and how the cost moved year by year.

## Run it

Requires Docker with Compose v2. No accounts, keys or database setup.

```sh
docker compose up --build
```

Then open **http://localhost:8088**. The first build takes a minute or two.
Click "Load sample mix" to load an illustrative example parcel set, or upload your
own CSV (columns below). Stop with `Ctrl+C`, then `docker compose down`.

Only port 8088 is published. nginx serves the built dashboard and proxies
`/api/` to the backend container, which is not reachable from the host. The
rate tables are baked into the backend image; the app never fetches anything
from the carriers at run time.

The API can be tried directly at **http://localhost:8088/api/docs** (Swagger
UI; "Try it out" on `POST /api/quote` is prefilled with the example from
[docs/API.md](docs/API.md)). The schema is at `/api/openapi.json`. The docs page
loads a pinned Swagger UI from jsDelivr, so it needs a network connection; the
dashboard does not.

## What it looks like

Captured by the Playwright suite against `docker compose up` with the sample
mix and the default controls (2026 prices, USPS commercial, $9.99 charged per
parcel). The review of every capture, desktop and phone, light and dark, is in
[docs/ux-review.md](docs/ux-review.md).

**Q1. One parcel.** A 3 lb box going 600 miles to a home costs $8.95 with USPS
Ground Advantage, $1.04 under the $9.99 the shop charges; FedEx Home Delivery
and UPS Ground cost about $30, partly because they bill the box at its 6 lb
dimensional weight. Each line item cites the rate file it came from.

![Q1: the cheapest service for one parcel with its line items](docs/screenshots/q1-quote-desktop.png)

**Q2. Breakevens.** For home delivery USPS Ground Advantage is cheapest at every
weight up to 450 miles (zones 1-4); from 800 miles on it stops winning at 27 lb,
UPS Ground or FedEx Home Delivery take over up to 50 lb, and USPS wins again
from 51 lb.

![Q2: weight by distance map of the cheapest ground carrier](docs/screenshots/q2-breakeven-desktop.png)

**Q3. The whole mix.** Shipping all 307 sample parcels with USPS costs $10.98 a
parcel against $31.14 with UPS and $31.22 with FedEx, yet the shop still loses
$0.31 a parcel: 40 % of parcels (124 of 307) ship at a loss even with the
cheapest carrier.

![Q3: blended cost per carrier, the loss verdict and the cost split](docs/screenshots/q3-blended-desktop.png)

**Q4. Year over year.** The same mix with USPS went from $9.17 in 2023 to $10.98
in 2026 (+19.7 %), 3.3 points more than the +16.4 % USPS announced over those
years; at its latest announced +6.5 % it would cost $11.70 in 2027.

![Q4: cost per parcel by year against the announced increases](docs/screenshots/q4-yearly-desktop.png)

**On a phone** the answer is on the first screen and the assumptions sit
behind "Adjust" (left: Q1; right: Q3 in dark mode).

<p>
  <img src="docs/screenshots/q1-quote-mobile.png" alt="Q1 on a 375 px phone" width="300">
  <img src="docs/screenshots/q3-blended-mobile-dark.png" alt="Q3 on a 375 px phone, dark mode" width="300">
</p>

## The problem and the user

The user is the owner of a small online shop that sends a few hundred to a few
thousand parcels a month, has no negotiated carrier contract, and therefore
pays published prices. The carrier is usually chosen by habit, and the flat
shipping price charged to customers was set once and not revisited.

The dashboard supports two decisions: which carrier and service to use for
each kind of parcel, and whether the flat shipping price still covers the real
cost once surcharges and yearly price increases are included.

### What it answers

| # | Question | The view |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | For one parcel (weight, size, distance, home or business, speed), which service is cheapest once surcharges are included, and what are the line items? | The cheapest total for the chosen speed comes first, with its saving over the next cheapest and the margin against your price. Below it, every carrier's cheapest service ranked, each with base rate, every surcharge (with its source page) and fuel. The form can start from one of your loaded parcels. |
| 2 | Where are the breakeven points between the Postal Service and FedEx or UPS ground, by weight and distance? | A weight by distance heatmap coloured by the cheapest ground carrier, for home or business delivery, with a heavy line where USPS stops winning and a plain-words readout of the breakeven weights; every cell's prices on hover, tap or focus, and the grid as a table. |
| 3 |

... [9590 characters truncated] ...

ggle that prices on December 10 of the year, not by
  ship date.
- UPS and FedEx bill a large or oversize package at a minimum of 90 lb. The
  minimum is modelled (issue #18), but actual weight is still capped at 70 lb;
  parcels whose dimensional weight is 71–150 lb are priced from the carriers'
  tables.
- Weight is capped at 70 lb, the USPS maximum. Alaska and Hawaii are out of
  scope.
- UPS 2024 and 2026 use the mid-year revision of the Daily Rates guide,
  because the December original could not be retrieved.

The same list is returned by `GET /api/meta` and shown in the app.

## What I would improve next

- #14 capture the UPS 2025-26 and 2026-27 demand surcharge documents.
- Real UPS and FedEx zones through their official rating APIs (with the
  shop's own API keys), replacing the distance estimate where keys are given.
- #13 script the source discovery so data collection runs end to end without

## Local development without Docker

Needs Python 3.12 with [uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/) and Node 20.12 or
newer (the image uses Node 22).

```sh
# backend on http://localhost:8787
cd backend && uv run uvicorn app.main:app --reload --port 8787

# frontend on http://localhost:5173, proxies /api to localhost:8787
cd frontend && npm ci && npm run dev
```

`VITE_API_TARGET` points the dev proxy elsewhere. `http://localhost:5173/?mock=1`
runs the frontend alone on recorded API responses. The API contract is in
[docs/API.md](docs/API.md), with example payloads in `docs/api-examples/`
(regenerated by `uv run --directory backend python scripts/export_api_examples.py`).

## Tests

GitHub Actions (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`) runs lint, the data and backend
suites, the frontend tests and build on Node 20.12, and `docker compose build`
on every push and pull request. A separate job on `main` fetches the PDFs
(cached by manifest checksum) and runs the data suite with
`PARCEL_RATES_REQUIRE_RAW=1`; it reports but does not block, since the archive
can be unreachable.

| Suite | Command | What it covers |
|---|---|---|
| Data | `uv run pytest data/tests` (repository root) | Schema, parsers against golden cells, table invariants, manifest; citations whose PDF is not fetched are skipped |
| Data, full | `python3 data/fetch_raw.py && PARCEL_RATES_REQUIRE_RAW=1 uv run pytest data/tests` | Same, with every citation checked against its PDF page; a missing PDF fails |
| Backend | `cd backend && uv run pytest` | Rate engine against hand-computed quotes, rule resolution, analysis and API |
| Frontend | `cd frontend && npm ci && npm test` | Components with Testing Library and the API contract test |
| Frontend types | `cd frontend && npm run typecheck` | |
| Lint | `uvx ruff check backend data` | pyflakes, core pycodestyle, import order, bugbear (config in `pyproject.toml`) |
| End to end | `docker compose up -d --build`, then `cd frontend && npx playwright install chromium && npm run e2e` | Playwright at 1440 × 900 and 375 × 812 (touch): the four questions, global controls, URL state, CSV upload, the phone settings sheet, and axe on every view in light and dark |
| Screenshots | same stack, `cd frontend && npm run e2e:update-screens` | Rewrites `frontend/e2e/screenshots/` (every view, both sizes, light and dark), reviewed in [docs/ux-review.md](docs/ux-review.md) |

The PDFs are not in git. Three tests need them: the citation check
(`test_citations.py`), the minimum-billable-weight sentences
(`test_min_billable.py`) and one UPS parser test on a real page. On a fresh
clone they are skipped with a message telling you to run
`python3 data/fetch_raw.py`; `PARCEL_RATES_REQUIRE_RAW=1` makes a missing PDF
a failure instead. The end-to-end suite targets `http://localhost:8088`; set
`E2E_BASE_URL` to run it elsewhere, for example against the dev servers
(`E2E_BASE_URL=http://localhost:5173 npm run e2e`).

## Repository layout

```
backend/            FastAPI app (app/), tests, scripts; Dockerfile
frontend/           Vite + React dashboard; Dockerfile and nginx config
data/raw/           manifest.csv, SOURCES.md per carrier, committed raw files
data/pipeline/      parsers and curated rules that write data/processed/
data/processed/     the rate tables the app loads
data/tests/         data tests and golden cells
docs/               decisions, API contract, data docs, agent log
docker-compose.yml  the reviewer run path
```

## How this was built: the agent log

The project was built with Claude Code, one GitHub issue per task, each in its
own branch and git worktree, merged through pull requests. Every session and
subagent transcript is exported to [docs/agent-log/](docs/agent-log/) as
Markdown by a `SessionEnd` hook (`.claude/hooks/export-session.sh`), with the
prompts, the agent's responses and its tool calls. Home directory paths are
redacted. [docs/DECISIONS.md](docs/DECISIONS.md) is the running log of the
decisions those conversations led to.
````

````
# Agent session log

Every Claude Code session run in this repository is exported here as Markdown
when the session ends, by the `SessionEnd` hook configured in
`.claude/settings.json` (`.claude/hooks/export-session.sh`).

- One file per session, named `<session-id>.md`. A resumed session keeps its
  id, so it overwrites its own earlier export.
- The export contains the full main transcript (user prompts, assistant text,
  thinking, tool calls and tool results) followed by one section per subagent
  transcript spawned from that session.
- The hook commits the exported file on its own (`docs(agent-log): export
  session <id>`), touching nothing else in the index.

To export the current session by hand:

```sh
printf '{"session_id":"%s","transcript_path":"%s","cwd":"%s","reason":"manual"}' \
  "$SID" "$HOME/.claude/projects/<encoded-project-path>/$SID.jsonl" "$PWD" \
  | .claude/hooks/export-session.sh
```

## Reading the logs

`tools/agent_log_html.py` turns these files into local HTML pages: an index of the
sessions, and per session the prompts in a sidebar, Claude's answers, collapsible
thinking and tool calls (each with its input and result), harness messages (background
tasks, subagent reports, local commands) as compact events, and the subagent transcripts.
Statistics (per session, and for all sessions on the index): calls per tool; the shell
commands and builtins the Bash calls ran (from a small tokenizer that skips heredoc bodies
and quoted text and counts what runs inside `$( … )`); and the subagents grouped by what they
did (reviewed a pull request, researched sources, fetched data, wrote Python, wrote
TypeScript / React, wrote tests, Docker/CI/docs), from their task, their tools and the files
they wrote, which is finer than their agent type (most are `general-purpose`).
It is a local viewer for people with access to the repository; nothing is published.

```sh
uv run tools/agent_log_html.py --open   # writes build/agent-log/ (git-ignored) and opens it
uv run --no-project --with markdown-it-py --with pytest pytest tools   # its tests
```
````

```
 README.md | 13 +++++++++++++

### #27
From docs/ux-review.md (PR for #8) and the final screenshot check:
- Q4 dims for ~250 ms while its second /api/yearly request runs on open; avoid the double request.
- Q4 driver bars use a colour close to UPS brown; pick a neutral that is not a carrier colour.
- Q2 band of thin UPS/FedEx slivers between 27 and 50 lb: consider merging near-ties (< $0.10) into a 'tie' style.
- One heatmap carrier label touches a cell edge on phones.
- Q3: very small surcharge kinds become slivers; fold under a threshold into 'other'.
- Q3: when the best mix equals one carrier, replace the duplicate 'Best mix' row with one line ('USPS wins every parcel').
- Mobile question tabs: letter-spacing too wide at 375 px.
### #14
UPS announced 2026-27 demand surcharges (from 2026-09-27 for Additional Handling, Large Package and Over Maximum; from 2026-10-25 for Ground Residential and Air, per trade press checked 2026-10-23), and the 2025-26 season update is also missing. The primary UPS PDFs are not in `data/raw/manifest.csv`: ups.com blocks the build host and the Wayback Machine was offline on 2026-10-23.

- [ ] Find the Wayback captures of the UPS demand-surcharge PDFs for both seasons (CDX query on `ups.com/assets/resources/webcontent/en_US/*demand*`).
- [ ] Add manifest rows and fetch with `data/fetch_raw.py --record`.
- [ ] Curate peak rows in `data/pipeline/ups_rules.py` with citations; remove the exception notes for 2025 and 2026.

Refs #2
### #13
**Why.** The survey in #1 located and downloaded the raw sources through one-off `curl` and WebFetch calls made by agents. Only the re-download step is scripted today (`data/fetch_raw.py` from `data/raw/manifest.csv`). The collection must be reproducible without any LLM, on any machine, so that the data can be refreshed later.

**Goal.** `data/collect.py` (or a small package under `data/collect/`) that rebuilds `data/raw/manifest.csv` and the raw files from scratch, with no agent in the loop.

**Tasks**
- [ ] Encode each carrier's document patterns in code, not in prose: USPS Notice 123 and price-file zips per price change (`pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive<date>/Notice123.pdf`, `pe.usps.com/resources/PriceChange/<name>`), USPS press releases, DMM 123/133, UPS Daily Rates guides and surcharge PDFs, FedEx Standard List Rates, surcharge-change PDFs, DAS lists, fuel pages, rate-change pages.
- [ ] Discovery through the Wayback CDX API where the live site blocks or overwrites files: query by URL prefix, pick the capture by rule (latest 200 capture before a cut-off date), and emit the `id_` passthrough URL. Record the rule so the choice is deterministic.
- [ ] Discovery of USPS price-change file names via the CDX prefix listing of `pe.usps.com/resources/PriceChange/*`.
- [ ] Polite by default: sequential, 3 to 5 s pause with jitter, backoff on 429 and 5xx, identifying User-Agent, resumable, never re-fetches a file whose checksum matches.
- [ ] Output: `manifest.csv` rows (carrier, year, path, url, method, sha256, bytes, in_git, content) and the files, then hand over to `fetch_raw.py` for the download or merge the two scripts.
- [ ] A `--check` mode that re-runs discovery and reports differences against the committed manifest (new captures, changed checksums) without downloading.
- [ ] README section "Data provenance" explains the full path: `python3 data/collect.py` then `python3 data/fetch_raw.py`, and that no step requires an agent.
- [ ] Test: discovery functions are unit-tested against recorded CDX responses (fixtures), so the tests run offline.

**Done when** a clean clone on another machine reproduces `data/raw/` and the manifest with the two commands, and the committed manifest matches the result.

Related: #1, #2.
Shell cwd was reset to ~/parcel-rates
```

```
name: data-collection-fully-scripted

Every step of data collection, including discovery of source URLs (e.g. Wayback CDX
lookups, file-name resolution), must live in committed scripts that run end to end
without an LLM or agent involved.

**Why:** On 2026-10-23 the user found that the parcel-rates survey agents had fetched
sources with one-off curl/WebFetch calls and only a post-hoc fetcher existed. Their rule:
the system must be independent of the agents used to build it, so the data can be
updated later or rebuilt on another computer without any LLM.

**How to apply:** When delegating data gathering, require the agent to deliver a script
(discovery + download + checksum manifest) rather than files alone; treat files without a
reproducing script as incomplete. Prefer polite sequential fetching with pauses.
```

```
import { FullQuestion } from "../FullQuestion";
import { useEffect, useId, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useApi, useLoad } from "../../api/ApiContext";
import type { Carrier, Meta, Parcel, YearlyResponse } from "../../api/types";
import type { ControlsState } from "../../state/urlState";
import { ChipGroup } from "../../components/ChipGroup";
import { LoadProblem } from "../../components/LoadProblem";
import { NeedParcels, type ParcelActions } from "../../components/NeedParcels";
import { useDebounced } from "../../hooks/useDebounced";
import { useLastData } from "../../hooks/useLastData";
import { carrierName, carrierVar } from "../../lib/carriers";
import { formatInt, formatUsd, signedPct, signedUsd } from "../../lib/format";
import { totalQuantity } from "../blended/logic";
import { YearChart } from "./YearChart";
import { DriverChart } from "./DriverChart";
import {
  DIMENSIONS,
  DIMENSION_LABEL,
  defaultIncrease,
  periodChange,
  pickCarrier,
  pointNotes,
  referenceYear,
  strongestDimension,
  type Dimension,
  type PointNote,
} from "./logic";

export interface YearlyPanelProps extends ParcelActions {
  controls: ControlsState;
  meta: Meta | null;
  parcels: Parcel[] | null;
}

export const INCREASE_MIN = -10;
export const INCREASE_MAX = 20;

export function YearlyPanel({ controls, parcels, onLoadSample, onUploadCsv }: YearlyPanelProps) {
  if (!parcels || parcels.length === 0) {
    return (
      <NeedParcels
        what="Load the sample mix or upload a CSV to see how the cost of your parcels moved under each year's published prices, against the increases the carriers announced, and which parcels drove it."
        onLoadSample={onLoadSample}
        onUploadCsv={onUploadCsv}
      />
    );
  }
  return <Yearly controls={controls} parcels={parcels} />;
}

function Yearly({ controls, parcels }: { controls: ControlsState; parcels: Parcel[] }) {
  const api = useApi();
  const [increase, setIncrease] = useState<number | null>(null);
  // false: each carrier's announced increase where it has one, the slider for the others; true: the slider for all.
  const [override, setOverride] = useState(false);
  const sent = useDebounced(increase, 250);
  const sentOverride = useDebounced(override, 250);
  const controlsKey = JSON.stringify(controls);
  const state = useLoad(
    (signal) =>
      api.yearly(
        {
          parcels,
          controls,
          ...(sent !== null ? { next_year_increase_percent: sent, next_year_override: sentOverride } : {}),
        },
        signal,
      ),
    [api, parcels, controlsKey, sent, sentOverride],
  );
  const data = useLastData(state);
  const total = useMemo(() => totalQuantity(parcels), [parcels]);

  // The slider starts at the pick carrier's latest announced increase, which the first answer tells us.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (increase !== null || !data) return;
    const ref = referenceYear(data);
    const d = ref !== null ? defaultIncrease(pickCarrier(data, ref)) : null;
    if (d !== null) setIncrease(d);
  }, [data, increase]);

  const loading = state.status === "loading";
  return (
    <div className="q4" aria-busy={loading}>
      {state.status === "error" && <LoadProblem error={state.error} />}
      {!data && loading && <p className="q-loading">Pricing {formatInt(total)} parcels under every year’s prices…</p>}
      {data && (
        <Body
          data={data}
          increase={increase}
          override={override}
          onIncrease={(v) => {
            setIncrease(v);
            setOverride(true);
          }}
          onReset={(v) => {
            setIncrease(v);
            setOverride(false);
          }}
          stale={loading}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function Body({
  data,
  increase,
  override,
  onIncrease,
  onReset,
  stale,
}: {
  data: YearlyResponse;
  increase: number | null;
  override: boolean;
  onIncrease: (v: number) => void;
  onReset: (v: number) => void;
  stale: boolean;
}) {
  const ref = referenceYear(data);
  const pick = ref !== null ? pickCarrier(data, ref) : null;
  const notes = pointNotes(data);
  if (!pick || ref === null) {
    return <p className="q-loading">No carrier could price these parcels in every year.</p>;
  }
  const change = periodChange(pick, ref);
  const others = data.carriers
    .filter((c) => c.carrier !== pick.carrier && c.parcels_used > 0)
    .map((c) => ({ c, ch: periodChange(c, ref) }))
    .filter((x) => x.ch !== null);

  return (
    <div className={stale ? "is-stale" : undefined}>
      <div className="answer q4-answer">
        <p className="kicker">
          {carrierName(pick.carrier)}, your cheapest carrier in {ref} · {formatInt(pick.parcels_used)} parcels
          {change && (
            <>
              {" "}
              · <span className="q4-since">change since {change.fromYear}</span>
            </>
          )}
        </p>
        {change ? (
          <>
            <p className="answer__figure answer__figure--stamp" data-testid="q4-answer">
              <span className="carrier-tag" style={{ backgroundColor: carrierVar(pick.carrier) }}>
                {carrierName(pick.carrier)}
```

```
# Quotes every parcel for every carrier and year without caching: about 1 s per
# 1,000 parcels on a laptop. Requests are capped at 10,000 parcels (api/models.py);
# a small shop's monthly mix of a few thousand parcels answers in a few seconds.
def yearly(
    tables: RateTables,
    parcels: list[Parcel],
    controls: Controls,
    next_year_increase_percent: float | None = None,
    next_year_override: bool = False,
) -> dict:
    result = []
    for carrier in tables.carriers():
        years = tables.years_for(carrier)
        per_year: dict[int, list[Quote | None]] = {}
        for y in years:
            cy = replace(controls, year=y, as_of=None)
            per_year[y] = per_parcel_quotes(tables, carrier, parcels, cy, year=y)

        # Like-for-like: only parcels the carrier can ship in every year.
        keep = [i for i in range(len(parcels)) if all(per_year[y][i] is not None for y in years)]
        used = [parcels[i] for i in keep]
        n = sum(p.quantity for p in used)
        excluded = sum(p.quantity for p in parcels) - n

        rows = []
        prev_y: int | None = None
        prev_cost: Decimal | None = None
        prev_conditions: set[str] = set()
        for y in years:
            conditions = conditions_in_force(tables, carrier, y, replace(controls, year=y, as_of=None))
            missing = sorted(prev_conditions - conditions)
            quotes = [per_year[y][i] for i in keep]
            totals = Totals()
            for p, q in zip(used, quotes):
                totals.add(q, p.quantity)
            cost = totals.total / n if n else ZERO
            services = sorted({q.service_id for q in quotes})
            row = {
                "year": y,
                "as_of": quotes[0].as_of.isoformat() if quotes else None,
                "cost_per_parcel": _r(cost),
                "summary": summarize(totals),
                "services_used": services,
                "change_vs_previous": None,
                "announced_increase": announced_increase(
                    tables, carrier, y, services, controls.usps_rate_card
                ),
                "drivers": None,
                "fuel": fuel_used(tables, carrier, y, controls),
                "warnings": (
                    [f"No {y} rules recorded for: {', '.join(missing)}; surcharges may be understated"]
                    if missing
                    else []
                ),
            }
            if prev_y is not None and n:
                diff = cost - prev_cost
                row["change_vs_previous"] = {
                    "from_year": prev_y,
                    "dollars_per_parcel": _r(diff),
                    "percent": _r(diff / prev_cost * 100, "0.01") if prev_cost else None,
                }
                prev_quotes = [per_year[prev_y][i] for i in keep]
                row["drivers"] = _drivers(tables, used, prev_quotes, quotes, n)
            rows.append(row)
            prev_y, prev_cost, prev_conditions = y, cost, conditions

        projected = None
        if rows and n:
            projected = project_next_year(
                tables,
                carrier,
                rows[-1],
                prev_cost,
                controls.usps_rate_card,
                next_year_increase_percent,
                next_year_override,
            )
        result.append(
            {
                "carrier": carrier,
                "parcels_used": n,
                "parcels_excluded": excluded,
                "years": rows,
                "projected": projected,
            }
        )
    return {"carriers": result}
```

```
def test_projection_uses_each_carriers_announced_next_year_increase(tables, sample):
    t = _with_ups_2027(tables)
    r = {c["carrier"]: c for c in yearly(t, sample[:40], Controls(year=2026), next_year_increase_percent=4)["carriers"]}
    ups = r["ups"]["projected"]
    assert (ups["year"], ups["increase_percent"], ups["basis"]) == (2027, 6.5, "announced")
    assert ups["source_files"] == ["ups/2027/rate-changes.html"]
    assert abs(ups["cost_per_parcel"] - r["ups"]["years"][-1]["cost_per_parcel"] * 1.065) <= 0.011
    # No announcement for USPS 2027 or FedEx 2028: the slider's percentage is an assumption.
    assert (r["usps"]["projected"]["year"], r["usps"]["projected"]["basis"]) == (2027, "assumed")
    assert (r["fedex"]["projected"]["year"], r["fedex"]["projected"]["increase_percent"]) == (2028, 4)
    assert r["fedex"]["projected"]["source_files"] == []


def test_projection_override_and_announced_without_a_slider(tables, sample):
    t = _with_ups_2027(tables)
    over = yearly(t, sample[:40], Controls(year=2026), next_year_increase_percent=3, next_year_override=True)
    assert {(c["projected"]["basis"], c["projected"]["increase_percent"]) for c in over["carriers"]} == {("override", 3)}
    by = {c["carrier"]: c["projected"] for c in over["carriers"]}
    assert by["ups"]["announced_percent"] == 6.5 and by["usps"]["announced_percent"] is None
    bare = {c["carrier"]: c["projected"] for c in yearly(t, sample[:40], Controls(year=2026))["carriers"]}
    assert bare["ups"]["basis"] == "announced" and bare["usps"] is None and bare["fedex"] is None


def test_yearly_cost_equals_blended_for_each_year(tables, sample):
    r = yearly(tables, sample, Controls(year=2026))
    for c in r["carriers"]:
        assert c["parcels_excluded"] == 0
        for row in c["years"]:
            b = blended(tables, sample, Controls(year=row["year"]))
            match = next(x for x in b["carriers"] if x["carrier"] == c["carrier"])
            assert row["cost_per_parcel"] == match["cost_per_parcel"]

### POST /api/yearly  (question 4)
Request `{ parcels: Parcel[], controls: Controls, next_year_increase_percent?: number, next_year_override?: boolean }`.
Response per carrier and year: blended cost per parcel under that year's
prices and rules, the year-over-year change, the carrier's announced average
increase for comparison, and the driver breakdown: for each parcel segment
(weight band, zone band, residential, speed), its contribution to the change
in dollars per parcel. Each carrier also gets a projected next year: the year
after its latest price list, at the increase the carrier announced for that
year when the data has one (`basis: "announced"`), otherwise at
`next_year_increase_percent` (`"assumed"`); with `next_year_override: true`
the percentage applies to every carrier (`"override"`). No announcement and no
percentage: `projected` is null.

Rules: each year is priced at its own base-rate effective date per carrier
(December 10 of that year when `peak`); `controls.as_of` and `controls.year`
are ignored. `fuel_override_percent` applies to every year. Only parcels the
carrier can ship in every one of its years are compared (`parcels_used`,
`parcels_excluded`). Years a carrier lacks are absent.

Shape: `YearlyResponse` in `backend/app/api/models.py`: `carriers[] {carrier,
parcels_used, parcels_excluded, years[], projected}`; `projected` is
`{year, increase_percent, cost_per_parcel, basis, announced_percent,
source_type, source_files}` or `null` (`announced_percent` is the carrier's own
figure for that year whatever the basis, null when none). Each year row:
`year`, `as_of`, `cost_per_parcel`, `summary` (the blended `Summary`),
`services_used`, `change_vs_previous {from_year, dollars_per_parcel, percent}`
(null for the first year), `announced_increase {percent, basis, services,
source_type}` where `basis` is `"all"` (the carrier-wide row), 
`"average_of_services"` (plain average of the rows for the services used,
listed in `services`) or `"none"`, `drivers` (per dimension a list of
  carrier: Carrier;
  parcels_used: number;
  parcels_excluded: number;
  years: YearRow[];
  projected: Projection | null;
}

export interface YearlyResponse {
  carriers: CarrierYears[];
}

/* ---------- errors ---------- */

/** One entry of a 422 body, as backend/app/api/errors.py writes it: always loc, msg and type. */
export interface ValidationIssue {
  loc: (string | number)[];
  msg: string; // written for the shop owner; parcel-list errors start "Parcel N: "
  type: string; // the Pydantic error type, e.g. "less_than_equal"
}

/** 422 body. */
export interface ValidationErrorBody {
  detail: ValidationIssue[];
}

/** FastAPI's own errors (404, 405) carry a plain string; the client accepts both. */
export interface HttpErrorBody {
  detail: string;
}
```

```
def test_yearly_drivers_sum_to_total_change(tables, sample):
    r = yearly(tables, sample, Controls(year=2026), next_year_increase_percent=4)
    by_carrier = {c["carrier"]: c for c in r["carriers"]}
    assert [y["year"] for y in by_carrier["ups"]["years"]] == tables.years_for("ups")
    assert 2027 in [y["year"] for y in by_carrier["fedex"]["years"]]
    for c in r["carriers"]:
        rows = c["years"]
        assert rows[0]["change_vs_previous"] is None and rows[0]["drivers"] is None
        for prev, row in zip(rows, rows[1:]):
            change = row["change_vs_previous"]["dollars_per_parcel"]
            assert abs((row["cost_per_parcel"] - prev["cost_per_parcel"]) - change) <= 0.011
            for dim in ("weight_band", "zone_band", "residential", "speed"):
                contributions = sum(d["change_per_parcel"] for d in row["drivers"][dim])
                assert abs(contributions - change) <= 0.01, (c["carrier"], row["year"], dim)
        last = rows[-1]["cost_per_parcel"]
        assert c["projected"]["year"] == rows[-1]["year"] + 1
        assert abs(c["projected"]["cost_per_parcel"] - last * 1.04) <= 0.011
```

```
def default_increase(carriers: list[dict]) -> float | None:
    """The next-year percentage used when the request gives none: the latest increase announced
    by the carrier cheapest in the reference year (the latest year every carrier has prices for,
    else the latest year any has). The UI's slider starts at this value."""
    priced = [c for c in carriers if c["years"] and c["parcels_used"]]
    if not priced:
        return None
    common = set.intersection(*({y["year"] for y in c["years"]} for c in priced))
    ref = max(common) if common else max(y["year"] for c in priced for y in c["years"])
    at_ref = [(row["cost_per_parcel"], c) for c in priced for row in c["years"] if row["year"] == ref]
    if not at_ref:
        return None
    pick = min(at_ref, key=lambda x: x[0])[1]
    return next(
        (y["announced_increase"]["percent"] for y in reversed(pick["years"]) if y["announced_increase"]["percent"] is not None),
        None,
    )
'''
s=s[:old_start]+new+s[old_end+len('    return {"carriers": result}\n'):]
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
```

````
# API fixtures

These JSON files are copies of `docs/api-examples/*.json`, real request/response pairs
generated by the backend (`backend/scripts/export_api_examples.py`); first on branch
`issue-3-engine-api`, commit `c6b8492` (PR #17), last regenerated for the new sample mix
(issue #22) , for order values and the free-shipping rule (issue #10), for the per-carrier projection (issue #11) and for ZIP zones (issue #9). Do not edit them by hand. When the
backend regenerates its examples, copy them over again:

```sh
cp ../docs/api-examples/*.json src/api/__fixtures__/
```

Two extra files cover years where not every carrier publishes prices: `breakeven-2027.json`
and `quote-2027.json` (2027 is FedEx only). They were produced by the backend as merged to main (PR #17,
merge 5785f6f) through FastAPI's `TestClient` (POST bodies inside each file), not by the export
script, so regenerate them the same way if the backend changes. They do not use the
sample mix; on issue #22 both were re-requested and still matched byte for byte.

Each file is `{ request: {method, path, body}, response: {status, body} }`.

They are used by:

- `src/api/contract.test.ts`, which validates every response body against the runtime
  guards in `src/api/guards.ts` (which follow `src/api/types.ts`), so drift between the
  backend and the frontend types fails the tests;
- `src/api/mocks.ts`, which serves these recorded responses in tests and in the
  `?mock=1` dev mode (not in production builds, where the chunk is left out). Mock responses are the recorded examples: they do not react to the
  request (a different parcel or year returns the same recorded answer).
````

```
function guard<T>(check: Check) {
  return (v: unknown): T => {
    check(v, "");
    return v as T;
  };
}

export const asHealth = guard<HealthResponse>(checks.health);
export const asMeta = guard<Meta>(checks.meta);
export const asSampleParcels = guard<SampleParcels>(checks.sampleParcels);
export const asQuoteResponse = guard<QuoteResponse>(checks.quote);
```

```
function Yearly({ controls, parcels }: { controls: ControlsState; parcels: Parcel[] }) {
  const api = useApi();
  // null until the owner moves the slider: the backend then applies its default
  // (next_year_default_percent), the same value the slider shows, so opening Q4 takes one request.
  const [increase, setIncrease] = useState<number | null>(null);
  // false: each carrier's announced increase where it has one, the slider for the others; true: the slider for all.
  const [override, setOverride] = useState(false);
  const sent = useDebounced(increase, 250);
  const sentOverride = useDebounced(override, 250);
  const controlsKey = JSON.stringify(controls);
  const state = useLoad(
    (signal) =>
      api.yearly(
        {
          parcels,
          controls,
          ...(sent !== null ? { next_year_increase_percent: sent, next_year_override: sentOverride } : {}),
        },
        signal,
      ),
    [api, parcels, controlsKey, sent, sentOverride],
  );
  const data = useLastData(state);
  const total = useMemo(() => totalQuantity(parcels), [parcels]);


  const loading = state.status === "loading";
  return (
    <div className="q4" aria-busy={loading}>
      {state.status === "error" && <LoadProblem error={state.error} />}
```

```
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { act, fireEvent, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { YearlyView } from "../YearlyView";
import { YearlyPanel } from "./YearlyPanel";
import { mockYearlyResponse } from "../../api/mocks";
import type { CarrierYears, Driver } from "../../api/types";
import { controls, meta, renderWith, sampleParcels, spyClient, stubResizeObserver } from "../blended/testUtils";
import {
  announcedPath,
  biggestFuelStep,
  defaultIncrease,
  driverSentence,
  fuelSentence,
  periodChange,
  pickCarrier,
  pointNotes,
  readableWarning,
  referenceYear,
  sortedDrivers,
  strongestDimension,
  withinChange,
  fastestSegment,
  projectionSentence,
} from "./logic";
import { toIndex } from "./YearChart";
import { niceTicks } from "./DriverChart";
import { parcelActions } from "../../test/parcelActions";

beforeEach(() => stubResizeObserver());

const carrier = (c: string) => mockYearlyResponse.carriers.find((x) => x.carrier === c) as CarrierYears;

describe("YearlyView", () => {
  it("asks for parcels when none are loaded", async () => {
    const { client, yearly } = spyClient();
    const onLoadSample = vi.fn();
    renderWith(client, <YearlyPanel controls={controls} meta={meta} parcels={null} {...parcelActions} onLoadSample={onLoadSample} />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Load the sample mix" }));
    expect(onLoadSample).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Upload CSV" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(yearly).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("answers with the cheapest carrier's change since the first year, against the announced increases", async () => {
    const { client } = spyClient();
    renderWith(client, <YearlyView controls={controls} meta={meta} parcels={sampleParcels} {...parcelActions} />);
    expect(await screen.findByTestId("q4-answer")).toHaveTextContent(/^USPS \+19\.7%$/);
    expect(screen.getByText(/your cheapest carrier in 2026/)).toHaveTextContent("· 307 parcels · change since 2023");
    const caption = screen.getByTestId("q4-caption");
    expect(caption).toHaveTextContent("$9.17 → $10.98 per parcel from 2023 to 2026 (+$1.81)");
    expect(caption).toHaveTextContent("compound to +16.4% over the same years");
    expect(caption).toHaveTextContent("3.3 points more than announced");
    expect(screen.getByText(/Same years for the others/)).toHaveTextContent("UPS +28.1% (announced +18.8%) · FedEx +27.8%");
  });

  it("starts the next-year slider at the latest announced increase and sends it", async () => {
    const { client, yearly } = spyClient();
    renderWith(client, <YearlyView controls={controls} meta={meta} parcels={sampleParcels} {...parcelActions} />);
    const slider = await screen.findByRole("slider", { name: /increase for 2027/i });
    await waitFor(() => expect(slider).toHaveValue("6.5"));
    expect(yearly.mock.calls[0]![0]).not.toHaveProperty("next_year_increase_percent");
    await waitFor(() => expect(yearly.mock.lastCall![0].next_year_increase_percent).toBe(6.5));
    expect(screen.getByTestId("q4-projection")).toHaveTextContent("$11.69 per parcel with USPS in 2027, from $10.98 in 2026 (+$0.71)");

    vi.useFakeTimers({ shouldAdvanceTime: true });
    fireEvent.change(slider, { target: { value: "10" } });
    expect(screen.getByTestId("q4-projection")).toHaveTextContent("$12.08");
    await act(async () => {
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(300);
    });
    vi.useRealTimers();
    await waitFor(() => expect(yearly.mock.lastCall![0].next_year_increase_percent).toBe(10));
    // Moving the slider overrides every carrier, announced or not; reset hands back to the announcements.
    expect(yearly.mock.lastCall![0].next_year_override).toBe(true);
    expect(screen.getByText(/your override, applied to every carrier/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Reset to +6.5%" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(yearly.mock.lastCall![0].next_year_override).toBe(false));
    expect(slider).toHaveValue("6.5");
  });

  it("says, for each carrier, which year it projects and on what basis", async () => {
    const { client } = spyClient();
    renderWith(client, <YearlyView controls={controls} meta={meta} parcels={sampleParcels} {...parcelActions} />);
    const basis = await screen.findByTestId("q4-projection-basis");
    expect(basis).toHaveTextContent("USPS 2027 +5.9% assumed · UPS 2027 +5.9% assumed · FedEx 2028 +5.9% assumed");
```

```
default on phones, where the dollar gap between USPS and the others flattens
the slopes. Points with a note carry a "!" badge; the notes are listed in
plain words under the chart. "How much of the change is fuel?" sits right
under the chart with a small per-year fuel table. The driver chart is a
diverging bar chart from zero: warm `--q4-up` when a segment made parcels
dearer, cool `--q4-down` when cheaper (neither is the loss/profit pair), sorted
by size. Each row label gives both the contribution to the total (what the
bars show, they add up to the change) and the change per parcel within the
segment ("each", `change_total / parcels`), so a small segment that rose fast
is not hidden by a big one; the sentence names the largest contributor and,
  the masthead is the only decoration.
- Consequences: the safety orange passes only as large text on white
  (3.6:1), so small loss text uses `--loss` (`#C2410C`, the same orange
  darkened). The Q2 heatmap's lightest saving step rose from 22 % to 45 % of
  the carrier colour, mixed in oklch with hue-true washes, so cells do not go
  pastel or beige on white. The Q4 "cost up" colour moved from rust to ochre
  to stay clear of the accent.

## 2026-10-23 — Compact page top

  using each ZIP's own point measured worse (92.3 % and 90.4 %), and reading
  the USPS chart zone as a UPS zone 89.8 %. The app uses prefix centres. When
  the UPS estimate is wrong it is usually one zone high (7.4 % of lanes
  against 1.7 % low), so estimated UPS prices lean slightly high.
- Typed miles still work; a parcel can give `destination_zip` (and
  `origin_zip`) instead, with "Ship from ZIP" as the shop's default origin.
  Alaska, Hawaii and the territories stay out of scope.

## Decisions changed after reviewing agent output

| Decision | What the agent produced | What changed, and why | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| Zone bands | My brief told the agents UPS/FedEx zone n = USPS zone n+1 | The USPS agent's DMM reading showed zones 1 and 2 are separate; bands rebuilt from the DMM | Agent log 2026-10-23 08:44; PR #15 |
| Rate-card naming | Amended schema stored UPS Daily Rates and FedEx Standard List Rates as `retail` | Owner: `list` for UPS and FedEx, `retail`/`commercial` for USPS, default commercial | Agent log 2026-10-23 08:10 and 08:18; PR #15 |
| Sample mix | Generator produced a mix with 71 % of parcels at a loss | Owner reversed it to a typical mix with expedited `price_charged` | Issue #22; PR #25 |
| Minimum billable weight | Engine priced oversize parcels at actual or dimensional weight | Review of PR #17 found the 90 lb minimum; added as cited data | Issue #18; PR #24 |

The delivery-area opt-in is not in this table: the transcript shows the agent
proposed it in the schema draft (agent log 2026-10-22 13:23) and the owner
accepted it unchanged, so it is recorded above as a decision, not a reversal.
```

```
def L(h):
  c=[int(h[i:i+2],16)/255 for i in (1,3,5)]; c=[x/12.92 if x<=0.03928 else ((x+0.055)/1.055)**2.4 for x in c]; return 0.2126*c[0]+0.7152*c[1]+0.0722*c[2]
def cr(a,b): a,b=L(a),L(b); return (max(a,b)+0.05)/(min(a,b)+0.05)
print('light up/paper',round(cr('#5f656b','#ffffff'),2),'up/down',round(cr('#5f656b','#2f6f86'),2))
print('dark up/paper',round(cr('#aab0b6','#0f1113'),2),'up/down',round(cr('#aab0b6','#6fb6cf'),2))"; git -C .. diff --stat
```

```
import { useMemo, useRef, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from "react";
import type { BreakevenCell, BreakevenResponse, Carrier, Meta } from "../../api/types";
import { CARRIERS } from "../../api/types";
import { cellGrid, groundName, savingLevel, zoneLabel } from "./readout";
import { useWidth } from "./useWidth";
import { CARRIER_NAME } from "../../lib/carriers";
import { formatInt, formatUsd } from "../../lib/format";

// The lightest step keeps 45 % of the carrier colour so cells read as the carrier, not as a pastel, on white.
const LEVEL_MIX = [45, 63, 82, 100] as const;

export function cellFill(c: BreakevenCell | null): string {
  if (!c?.winner) return "var(--wash)";
  const v = `var(--carrier-${c.winner.carrier})`;
  const w = `var(--carrier-${c.winner.carrier}-wash)`;
  return `color-mix(in oklch, ${v} ${LEVEL_MIX[savingLevel(c.saving)]}%, ${w})`;
}

function cellLabel(c: BreakevenCell | null, meta: Meta | null, residential: boolean): string {
  if (!c) return "no data";
  const head = `${c.weight_lb} lb, ${formatInt(c.distance_miles)} mi`;
  if (!c.winner) return `${head}: no ground service can carry it`;
  const win = `${groundName(meta, c.winner.carrier, residential)} cheapest at ${formatUsd(c.winner.total)}`;
  const run = c.runner_up
    ? `, ${formatUsd(c.saving)} less than ${groundName(meta, c.runner_up.carrier, residential)}`
    : ", no other carrier to compare";
  return `${head}: ${win}${run}`;
}

const AXIS_W = 46;
const HEAD_H = 38;

export interface HeatmapProps {
  res: BreakevenResponse;
  meta: Meta | null;
}

export function Heatmap({ res, meta }: HeatmapProps) {
  const grid = useMemo(() => cellGrid(res), [res]);
  const rows = res.weights_lb.length;
  const cols = res.distances_miles.length;
  const [wrapRef, width] = useWidth<HTMLDivElement>(640);
  const compact = width < 480;
  const colW = Math.max(30, (Math.max(width, 280) - AXIS_W) / Math.max(cols, 1));
  const rowH = rows > 40 ? 8 : compact ? 18 : 22;
  const svgW = AXIS_W + colW * cols;
  const svgH = HEAD_H + rowH * rows + 2;

  const [active, setActive] = useState({ r: 0, c: 0 });
  const [hover, setHover] = useState<{ r: number; c: number } | null>(null);
  const [focused, setFocused] = useState(false);
  const [pinned, setPinned] = useState(false);
  /** Escape closes the tooltip but keeps the focus ring; any move or click opens it again. */
  const [tipDismissed, setTipDismissed] = useState(false);
  const cellRefs = useRef<(SVGRectElement | null)[]>([]);

  const ring = hover ?? (focused || pinned ? active : null);
  const show = hover ?? (!tipDismissed && (focused || pinned) ? active : null);
  const shownCell = show ? (grid[show.r]?.[show.c] ?? null) : null;

  const moveTo = (r: number, c: number) => {
    const nr = Math.min(rows - 1, Math.max(0, r));
    const nc = Math.min(cols - 1, Math.max(0, c));
    setActive({ r: nr, c: nc });
    setHover(null);
    setTipDismissed(false);
    cellRefs.current[nr * cols + nc]?.focus();
  };

  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    const { r, c } = active;
    const map: Record<string, [number, number] | undefined> = {
      ArrowUp: [r - 1, c],
      ArrowDown: [r + 1, c],
      ArrowLeft: [r, c - 1],
      ArrowRight: [r, c + 1],
      PageUp: [r - 10, c],
      PageDown: [r + 10, c],
      Home: e.ctrlKey ? [0, 0] : [r, 0],
      End: e.ctrlKey ? [rows - 1, cols - 1] : [r, cols - 1],
    };
    if (e.key === "Escape") {
      setPinned(false);
      setHover(null);
      setTipDismissed(true);
      return;
    }
    const to = map[e.key];
    if (!to) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    setFocused(true);
    moveTo(to[0], to[1]);
  };

  // Boundaries where the winner changes: drawn in ink so regions read without colour.
  const deco = useMemo(() => decorations(grid, res.weights_lb, cols, colW, rowH), [grid, res.weights_lb, cols, colW, rowH]);
  const { boundaries, rivalEdges, runLabels, weightTicks, colSeps } = deco;

  const tip = show && shownCell ? tooltipPos(show, colW, rowH, svgW) : null;

  return (
    <div className="hm" ref={wrapRef}>
      <div className="hm__scroll">
        <svg
          className="hm__svg"
          width={svgW}
          height={svgH}
          viewBox={`0 0 ${svgW} ${svgH}`}
          role="grid"
          aria-label={`Cheapest ground carrier by weight (rows, ${res.weights_lb[0]} to ${res.weights_lb[rows - 1]} lb) and distance (columns). Arrow keys move between cells.`}
          aria-rowcount={rows}
          aria-colcount={cols}
          onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
          onPointerLeave={() => setHover(null)}
        >
          <g aria-hidden="true" className="hm__axis">
            {res.distances_miles.map((d, c) => {
              const band = res.breakeven_weight_lb[c];
              const x = AXIS_W + c * colW + colW / 2;
              return (
                <g key={d}>
                  <text x={x} y={14} textAnchor="middle" className="hm__colhead">
                    {compact ? formatInt(d) : `${formatInt(d)} mi`}
                  </text>
                  <text x={x} y={29} textAnchor="middle" className="hm__colsub">
                    {compact ? `z${band?.zone_usps ?? "—"}` : `zone ${band?.zone_usps ?? "—"}`}
                  </text>
                </g>
              );
            })}
            {weightTicks.map(({ w, r }) => (
              <text key={w} x={AXIS_W - 6} y={HEAD_H + r * rowH + rowH / 2} dy="0.35em" textAnchor="end" className="hm__rowhead">
                {w} lb
              </text>
            ))}
          </g>
          {res.weights_lb.map((w, r) => (
            <g key={w} role="row" aria-rowindex={r + 1}>
              {res.distances_miles.map((d, c) => {
                const cell = grid[r]?.[c] ?? null;
                const isActive = active.r === r && active.c === c;
                return (
                  <rect
                    key={d}
                    ref={(el) => {
                      cellRefs.current[r * cols + c] = el;
                    }}
                    role="gridcell"
                    aria-colindex={c + 1}
                    aria-label={cellLabel(cell, meta, res.residential)}
                    aria-selected={isActive}
                    tabIndex={isActive ? 0 : -1}
                    data-carrier={cell?.winner?.carrier ?? "none"}
                    data-level={cell ? savingLevel(cell.saving) : 0}
                    x={AXIS_W + c * colW}
                    y={HEAD_H + r * rowH}
                    width={colW}
                    height={rowH}
                    style={{ fill: cellFill(cell) }}
                    className="hm__cell"
                    onPointerEnter={(e) => {
                      if (e.pointerType !== "mouse") return;
                      setHover({ r, c });
                      setTipDismissed(false);
                    }}
                    onClick={() => {
                      setActive({ r, c });
                      setPinned(true);
                      setTipDismissed(false);
                    }}
                    onFocus={() => {
                      setActive({ r, c });
                      setFocused(true);
                    }}
                    onBlur={() => setFocused(false)}
                  />
                );
              })}
            </g>
          ))}
          <path
            d={colSeps}
            className="hm__colsep"
            aria-hidden="true"
          />
          <path d={rivalEdges} className="hm__boundary hm__boundary--rival" aria-hidden="true" />
          <path d={boundaries} className="hm__boundary" aria-hidden="true" />
          <g aria-hidden="true">
            {runLabels.map((l, i) => (
              <text key={i} x={l.x} y={l.y} dy="0.35em" textAnchor="middle" className="hm__runlabel">
                {CARRIER_NAME[l.carrier]}
              </text>
            ))}
          </g>
          {ring && (
            <rect
              aria-hidden="true"
              data-testid="heatmap-focus"
              className="hm__focus"
              x={AXIS_W + ring.c * colW + 1}
```

```
/**
 * The static layers over the cells: winner boundaries (USPS against the rest heavy, UPS against
 * FedEx thin), carrier names on tall runs, weight ticks and column separators. Pure, so the view
 * memoizes it and hovering a cell does not recompute it.
 */
export function decorations(
  grid: (BreakevenCell | null)[][],
  weights: readonly number[],
  cols: number,
  colW: number,
  rowH: number,
) {
  const rows = weights.length;
  const boundaries: string[] = [];
  const rivalEdges: string[] = [];
  const edge = (a: Carrier | undefined, b: Carrier | undefined) => (a === "usps" || b === "usps" ? boundaries : rivalEdges);
  for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
    for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
      const here = grid[r]?.[c]?.winner?.carrier;
      const right = grid[r]?.[c + 1]?.winner?.carrier;
      const below = grid[r + 1]?.[c]?.winner?.carrier;
      const x = AXIS_W + c * colW;
      const y = HEAD_H + r * rowH;
      if (c + 1 < cols && here !== right) edge(here, right).push(`M${x + colW},${y}v${rowH}`);
      if (r + 1 < rows && here !== below) edge(here, below).push(`M${x},${y + rowH}h${colW}`);
    }
  }

  const runLabels: { x: number; y: number; carrier: Carrier }[] = [];
  for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
    let start = 0;
    for (let r = 1; r <= rows; r++) {
      const prev = grid[r - 1]?.[c]?.winner?.carrier;
      const cur = r < rows ? grid[r]?.[c]?.winner?.carrier : undefined;
      if (cur === prev && r < rows) continue;
      if (prev && (r - start) * rowH >= 26) {
        runLabels.push({ x: AXIS_W + c * colW + colW / 2, y: HEAD_H + ((start + r) / 2) * rowH, carrier: prev });
      }
      start = r;
    }
  }

  const weightTicks = weights.map((w, r) => ({ w, r })).filter(({ w, r }) => (rows > 40 ? r === 0 || w % 10 === 0 : true));
  const colSeps = Array.from({ length: Math.max(0, cols - 1) }, (_, c) => `M${AXIS_W + (c + 1) * colW},${HEAD_H}v${rowH * rows}`).join("");
  return { boundaries: boundaries.join(""), rivalEdges: rivalEdges.join(""), runLabels, weightTicks, colSeps };
}

function tooltipPos(at: { r: number; c: number }, colW: number, rowH: number, svgW: number) {
  const TIP_W = 220;
  const cx = AXIS_W + at.c * colW + colW / 2;
  const left = Math.min(Math.max(4, cx - TIP_W / 2), Math.max(4, svgW - TIP_W - 4));
  const y = HEAD_H + at.r * rowH;
  const below = y < 170;
  return { left, top: below ? y + rowH + 8 : y - 8, below };
}

export function Tooltip({ cell, meta, residential }: { cell: BreakevenCell; meta: Meta | null; residential: boolean }) {
  const entries = CARRIERS.filter((c) => cell.totals[c] !== undefined).sort((a, b) => cell.totals[a]! - cell.totals[b]!);
  return (
    <>
      <p className="hm__tiphead">
        {cell.weight_lb} lb · {formatInt(cell.distance_miles)} mi
        <span>{zoneLabel(cell.zone_usps, cell.zone_private)}</span>
      </p>
      <table className="hm__tiptable">
        <tbody>
          {entries.map((c) => (
            <tr key={c} className={cell.winner?.carrier === c ? "is-winner" : undefined}>
              <th scope="row">
                <span className={`hm__dot hm__dot--${c}`} aria-hidden="true" />
                {groundName(meta, c, residential)}
              </th>
              <td className="num">{formatUsd(cell.totals[c]!)}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <p className="hm__tipfoot">
        {cell.winner && cell.runner_up
          ? `${CARRIER_NAME[cell.winner.carrier]} saves ${formatUsd(cell.saving)} over ${CARRIER_NAME[cell.runner_up.carrier]}`
          : cell.winner
            ? `Only ${CARRIER_NAME[cell.winner.carrier]} has a price here`
            : "No ground service can carry it"}
      </p>
    </>
  );
}
```
