```
import { FullQuestion } from "./FullQuestion";
import { useId, useState } from "react";
import { useApi, useLoad } from "../api/ApiContext";
import type { BreakevenResponse, Carrier } from "../api/types";
import { CARRIERS } from "../api/types";
import { BreakevenTable } from "./breakeven/BreakevenTable";
import { Heatmap } from "./breakeven/Heatmap";
import { SAVING_LABELS, groundName, readout } from "./breakeven/readout";
import type { ViewProps } from "./types";
import "../styles/views.css";
import "./breakeven/breakeven.css";
import { CARRIER_NAME, carriersForYear, missingCarriersNote } from "../lib/carriers";

function Legend({ res, meta }: { res: BreakevenResponse; meta: ViewProps["meta"] }) {
  const winners = CARRIERS.filter((c) => res.cells.some((x) => x.winner?.carrier === c));
  return (
    <div className="hml" aria-label="Legend" role="group">
      <ul className="hml__carriers">
        {winners.map((c: Carrier) => (
          <li key={c}>
            <span className={`hml__sw hml__sw--${c}`} aria-hidden="true" />
            <strong>{CARRIER_NAME[c]}</strong> {groundName(meta, c, res.residential).replace(new RegExp(`^${CARRIER_NAME[c]} `), "")}{" "}
            cheapest
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <div className="hml__ramp">
        <span className="hml__ramplabel">Saving over the next cheapest:</span>
        <ol>
          {SAVING_LABELS.map((l, i) => (
            <li key={l}>
              <span className={`hml__step hml__step--${i}`} aria-hidden="true" />
              {l}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      </div>
      <p className="hml__note">A heavy line marks where USPS stops or starts winning; a dotted line where UPS and FedEx swap. Tap, hover or focus a cell for every price.</p>
    </div>
  );
}

export function BreakevenView({ controls, meta }: ViewProps) {
  const api = useApi();
  const [residential, setResidential] = useState(true);
  const [asTable, setAsTable] = useState(false);
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);
  const uid = useId();
  const key = JSON.stringify({ controls, residential });
  const state = useLoad<BreakevenResponse>((signal) => api.breakeven({ controls, residential }, signal), [api, key, attempt]);
```

```
 * map they made a band of thin alternating slivers. USPS cells are never merged, since where
 * USPS stops winning is the question the map answers. */
export const TIE_DOLLARS = 0.1;

export function isTie(c: BreakevenCell | null): boolean {
  const a = c?.winner?.carrier;
  const b = c?.runner_up?.carrier;
  return !!a && !!b && a !== "usps" && b !== "usps" && c!.saving < TIE_DOLLARS;
}

/** What a cell shows: its winner, or "tie" for a UPS/FedEx near-tie. */
export const cellKey = (c: BreakevenCell | null): Carrier | "tie" | undefined => (isTie(c) ? "tie" : c?.winner?.carrier);

export function savingLevel(''',1)
```

```
  const { winner, runner_up: runnerUp } = cell;
  if (!winner) return "No ground service can carry it";
  if (!runnerUp) return `Only ${CARRIER_NAME[winner.carrier]} has a price here`;
  const saves = `${CARRIER_NAME[winner.carrier]} saves ${formatUsd(cell.saving)} over ${CARRIER_NAME[runnerUp.carrier]}`;
  return isTie(cell) ? `A tie: ${saves}, under ${formatUsd(TIE_DOLLARS)}` : saves;
}
```

```
import { Bar, BarChart, CartesianGrid, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip, XAxis, YAxis } from "recharts";
import type { BlendedResponse } from "../../api/types";
import { OTHER_KIND, splitRows, surchargeKinds, type SplitRow } from "./logic";
import { useMediaQuery } from "../../hooks/useMediaQuery";
import { ChartFrame } from "../../components/ChartFrame";
import { useCssColors } from "../../hooks/useCssColors";
import { CARRIER_NAME } from "../../lib/carriers";
import { conditionLabel, formatShare, formatUsd } from "../../lib/format";

const COLOR_VARS = {
  base: "--q3-base",
  s0: "--q3-sur-1",
  s1: "--q3-sur-2",
  s2: "--q3-sur-3",
  s3: "--q3-sur-4",
  fuel: "--q3-fuel",
  ink: "--ink",
  muted: "--ink-muted",
  rule: "--rule",
  sheet: "--sheet",
  paper: "--paper",
  usps: "--carrier-usps",
  ups: "--carrier-ups",
  fedex: "--carrier-fedex",
} as const;

interface Segment {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  colorKey: keyof typeof COLOR_VARS;
  value: (r: SplitRow) => number;
}

function segmentsFor(kinds: { shown: string[]; folded: string[] }): Segment[] {
  const sur: Segment[] = kinds.shown.map((k, i) => ({
    key: `k_${k}`,
    label: conditionLabel(k),
    colorKey: (["s0", "s1", "s2", "s3"] as const)[Math.min(i, 3)]!,
    value: (r) => r.kinds[k] ?? 0,
  }));
  if (kinds.folded.length)
    sur.push({ key: "k_other", label: "Other surcharges", colorKey: "s3", value: (r) => r.kinds[OTHER_KIND] ?? 0 });
  return [
    { key: "base", label: "Base rate", colorKey: "base", value: (r) => r.base },
    ...sur,
    { key: "fuel", label: "Fuel", colorKey: "fuel", value: (r) => r.fuel },
  ];
}

export function CostSplitChart({ data }: { data: BlendedResponse }) {
  const colors = useCssColors(COLOR_VARS);
  const narrow = useMediaQuery("(max-width: 39.999rem)");
  const kinds = surchargeKinds(data);
  const rows = splitRows(data, kinds, CARRIER_NAME);
  const segs = segmentsFor(kinds);
  const chartRows = rows.map((r) => {
    const o: Record<string, number | string> = { label: r.label, carrier: r.carrier };
    for (const s of segs) o[s.key] = s.value(r);
    return o;
  });
  const byLabel = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.label, r]));
  const heaviest = [...rows].sort((a, b) => b.surchargeShare + b.fuelShare - (a.surchargeShare + a.fuelShare))[0];

  const summary = (
    <>
      {rows.map((r, i) => (
        <span key={r.carrier}>
          {i > 0 && "; "}
          {r.label} {formatUsd(r.total)}: base {formatUsd(r.base)}, surcharges {formatUsd(r.surcharges)} (
          {formatShare(r.surchargeShare)}), fuel {formatUsd(r.fuel)} ({formatShare(r.fuelShare)})
        </span>
      ))}
      .
      {heaviest && heaviest.surchargeShare + heaviest.fuelShare > 0 && (
        <>
          {" "}
          Add-ons weigh most on {heaviest.label}: {formatShare(heaviest.surchargeShare + heaviest.fuelShare)} of its cost is
          surcharges and fuel rather than base rate.
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );

  const yWidth = narrow ? 104 : 168;
  const tick = (props: { x?: number | string; y?: number | string; payload?: { value?: string } }) => {
    const y = Number(props.y ?? 0);
    const label = props.payload?.value ?? "";
    const r = byLabel.get(label);
    if (!r) return <g />;
    return (
      <g transform={`translate(4,${y})`}>
        <text x={0} y={-4} fill={colors[r.carrier]} fontWeight={700} fontSize={14} fontFamily="var(--font-ui)">
          {label} {formatUsd(r.total)}
        </text>
        <text x={0} y={13} fill={colors.muted} fontSize={12} fontFamily="var(--font-ui)">
          {narrow ? `surch. ${formatShare(r.surchargeShare)}` : `surcharges ${formatShare(r.surchargeShare)} · fuel ${formatShare(r.fuelShare)}`}
        </text>
      </g>
    );
  };

  const chart = (
    <>
      <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={rows.length * 64 + 40} initialDimension={{ width: 600, height: 240 }}>
        <BarChart accessibilityLayer={false} data={chartRows} layout="vertical" margin={{ top: 4, right: 12, bottom: 4, left: 0 }} barCategoryGap="28%">
          <CartesianGrid horizontal={false} stroke={colors.rule} />
          <XAxis
            type="number"
            tickFormatter={(v: number) => `$${v}`}
            tick={{ fill: colors.muted, fontSize: 12 }}
            axisLine={false}
            tickLine={false}
          />
          <YAxis type="category" dataKey="label" width={yWidth} tick={tick} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} />
          <Tooltip
            cursor={{ fill: colors.rule, opacity: 0.35 }}
            contentStyle={{ background: colors.sheet, border: `1px solid ${colors.rule}`, borderRadius: 2, fontSize: 13 }}
            labelStyle={{ color: colors.ink, fontWeight: 700 }}
            formatter={(v, name) => [formatUsd(Number(v)), name]}
          />
          {segs.map((s) => (
            <Bar
              key={s.key}
              dataKey={s.key}
              name={s.label}
              stackId="cost"
              fill={colors[s.colorKey]}
              stroke={colors.paper}
              strokeWidth={2}
              isAnimationActive={false}
            />
          ))}
        </BarChart>
      </ResponsiveContainer>
      <ul className="legend" aria-hidden="true">
        {segs.map((s) => (
          <li key={s.key}>
            <span className="legend__swatch" style={{ background: `var(${COLOR_VARS[s.colorKey]})` }} />
            {s.label}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </>
  );

  const table = (
    <table className="ledger-table">
      <caption className="visually-hidden">Cost per parcel by part, per carrier</caption>
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th scope="col">Carrier</th>
          {segs.map((s) => (
            <th key={s.key} scope="col" className="num">
              {s.label}
            </th>
          ))}
          <th scope="col" className="num">
            Total
          </th>
          <th scope="col" className="num">
            Surcharge share
          </th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {rows.map((r) => (
          <tr key={r.carrier}>
            <th scope="row">{r.label}</th>
            {segs.map((s) => (
              <td key={s.key} className="num">
                {formatUsd(s.value(r))}
              </td>
            ))}
            <td className="num">{formatUsd(r.total)}</td>
            <td className="num">{formatShare(r.surchargeShare)}</td>
          </tr>
        ))}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );

  return <ChartFrame title="What each parcel costs, by part" summary={summary} chart={chart} table={table} />;
}
```

```
/* ---------- stacked split ---------- */

export const MAX_KINDS = 3;
export const OTHER_KIND = "__other";

/** Surcharge kinds ordered by their total across carriers; the top MAX_KINDS get their own segment. */
export function surchargeKinds(r: BlendedResponse): { shown: string[]; folded: string[] } {
  const totals = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const c of r.carriers)
    for (const s of c.surcharges_by_condition) totals.set(s.condition, (totals.get(s.condition) ?? 0) + s.total);
  const order = [...totals.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).map(([k]) => k);
  if (order.length <= MAX_KINDS + 1) return { shown: order, folded: [] };
  return { shown: order.slice(0, MAX_KINDS), folded: order.slice(MAX_KINDS) };
}

export interface SplitRow {
  carrier: Carrier;
  label: string;
  base: number;
  fuel: number;
```

```
export function splitRows(r: BlendedResponse, kinds: { shown: string[]; folded: string[] }, names: Record<Carrier, string>): SplitRow[] {
  return r.carriers
    .filter((c) => c.parcels_shipped > 0)
    .map((c) => {
      const byKind: Record<string, number> = {};
      for (const k of kinds.shown) byKind[k] = 0;
      if (kinds.folded.length) byKind[OTHER_KIND] = 0;
      for (const s of c.surcharges_by_condition) {
        const key = kinds.shown.includes(s.condition) ? s.condition : OTHER_KIND;
        byKind[key] = round2((byKind[key] ?? 0) + s.per_parcel);
      }
      return {
        carrier: c.carrier,
        label: names[c.carrier],
        base: c.split.base.per_parcel,
        fuel: c.split.fuel.per_parcel,
        kinds: byKind,
        surcharges: c.split.surcharges.per_parcel,
        total: c.cost_per_parcel,
        surchargeShare: c.split.surcharges.share,
        fuelShare: c.split.fuel.share,
      };
    });
}
```

```
s=open(p).read()
old=s[s.index('export const MAX_KINDS = 3;'):s.index('export interface SplitRow {')]
s=s.replace(old,'''export const MAX_KINDS = 3;
export const OTHER_KIND = "__other";
/** A kind narrower than this share of the longest bar is a sliver: it goes under "Other". */
export const MIN_KIND_SHARE = 0.02;

/** Surcharge kinds ordered by their total across carriers. A kind gets its own segment when, for
 * some carrier, it is at least MIN_KIND_SHARE of the most expensive carrier's cost per parcel; at
 * most MAX_KINDS do, and the rest fold into OTHER_KIND. */
export function surchargeKinds(r: BlendedResponse): { shown: string[]; folded: string[] } {
  const totals = new Map<string, number>();
  const widest = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const c of r.carriers)
    for (const s of c.surcharges_by_condition) {
      totals.set(s.condition, (totals.get(s.condition) ?? 0) + s.total);
      widest.set(s.condition, Math.max(widest.get(s.condition) ?? 0, s.per_parcel));
    }
  const longest = Math.max(0, ...r.carriers.filter((c) => c.parcels_shipped > 0).map((c) => c.cost_per_parcel));
  const order = [...totals.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).map(([k]) => k);
  const visible = order.filter((k) => (widest.get(k) ?? 0) >= MIN_KIND_SHARE * longest);
  const slivers = order.filter((k) => !visible.includes(k));
  // Folding a single kind would only rename it, so up to MAX_KINDS + 1 visible kinds all stay when none fold.
  const room = slivers.length ? MAX_KINDS : MAX_KINDS + 1;
  if (visible.length <= room) return { shown: visible, folded: slivers };
  return { shown: visible.slice(0, MAX_KINDS), folded: [...visible.slice(MAX_KINDS), ...slivers] };
}

/** Every kind on its own, for the table, where a small amount is still readable. */
export const allKinds = (r: BlendedResponse) => {
  const { shown, folded } = surchargeKinds(r);
  return { shown: [...shown, ...folded], folded: [] };
};
```

```
function Answer({ data, answer, kicker }: { data: BlendedResponse; answer: BlendedAnswer; kicker: string }) {
  const { cheapest, others, mixSavingPerParcel, mixSavingTotal } = answer;
  const mix = data.best_mix;
  const mixCounts = Object.entries(mix.parcels_by_carrier)
    .filter(([, n]) => n > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
  return (
    <div className="answer q3-answer">
      <p className="kicker">{kicker}</p>
      <p className="answer__figure answer__figure--stamp" data-testid="q3-answer">
        <span className="carrier-tag" style={{ backgroundColor: carrierVar(cheapest.carrier) }}>
          {carrierName(cheapest.carrier)}
        </span>{" "}
        {formatUsd(cheapest.cost_per_parcel)}
        <span className="answer__unit"> per parcel</span>
      </p>
      <p className="answer__caption">
        Blended over every parcel at its cheapest {carrierName(cheapest.carrier)} service for the speed it needs.
        {others.length > 0 && " "}
        {others.map((c, i) => (
          <span key={c.carrier}>
            {i > 0 && ", "}
            <strong style={{ color: carrierVar(c.carrier) }}>{carrierName(c.carrier)}</strong> {formatUsd(c.cost_per_parcel)}{" "}
            ({signedUsd(c.cost_per_parcel - cheapest.cost_per_parcel)})
          </span>
        ))}
        {others.length > 0 && "."}
      </p>
      <FullQuestion id="blended" />
      <div className="mix" data-testid="q3-mix">
        <p className="mix__line">
          <span className="mix__label">Best mix</span>{" "}
          <span className="figure mix__figure">{formatUsd(mix.cost_per_parcel)}</span> per parcel, choosing the cheapest
          carrier for each parcel:{" "}
          {mixSavingPerParcel > 0 ? (
            <>
              <strong>{formatUsd(mixSavingPerParcel)} less per parcel</strong> than {carrierName(cheapest.carrier)} alone (
              {formatUsd(mixSavingTotal)} on these parcels).
            </>
          ) : (
            <>no saving over {carrierName(cheapest.carrier)} alone.</>
          )}
        </p>
        <p className="mix__split">
          {mixCounts.map(([c, n], i) => (
            <span key={c}>
              {i > 0 && " · "}
              <span style={{ color: carrierVar(c as Carrier) }}>{carrierName(c)}</span> {formatInt(n)}
            </span>
          ))}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Verdict({
  data,
  answer,
  controls,
  hasOrderValues,
}: {
  data: BlendedResponse;
  answer: BlendedAnswer;
  controls: ControlsState;
  hasOrderValues: boolean;
}) {
  const c = answer.cheapest;
  const loss = c.margin_per_parcel < 0;
  const charged = data.revenue_per_parcel;
  const mixedPrices = Math.abs(charged - controls.flat_price) >= 0.005;
  const rows = [
    ...[...data.carriers].filter((x) => x.parcels_shipped > 0).sort((a, b) => a.cost_per_parcel - b.cost_per_parcel),
  ];
  return (
    <section className={`verdict ${loss ? "verdict--loss" : "verdict--profit"}`} aria-labelledby="q3-verdict">
      <h3 id="q3-verdict" className="verdict__q">
        Does your {formatUsd(controls.flat_price)} shipping price cover the cost?
      </h3>
      <p className="verdict__figure" data-testid="q3-verdict">
        <span className="verdict__word">{loss ? "No." : "Yes."}</span>{" "}
        {loss ? (
          <>
            You lose {formatUsd(-c.margin_per_parcel)} per parcel with {carrierName(c.carrier)}
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            {formatUsd(c.margin_per_parcel)} margin per parcel with {carrierName(c.carrier)}
          </>
        )}
      </p>
      <p className="verdict__loss-share" data-testid="q3-loss-share">
        {c.parcels_at_loss === 0 ? (
          <>
            Every parcel covers its cost with {carrierName(c.carrier)}
            {data.best_mix.parcels_at_loss > 0 && <>; {formatShare(data.best_mix.loss_share)} would lose money with the best mix</>}.
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <strong>{formatShare(c.loss_share)}</strong> of parcels ({formatInt(c.parcels_at_loss)} of {formatInt(c.parcels_shipped)})
            ship at a loss even with the cheapest carrier
            {data.best_mix.loss_share !== c.loss_share && <>; {formatShare(data.best_mix.loss_share)} with the best mix</>}.
          </>
        )}
      </p>
      {data.free_shipping && <FreeShippingNote free={data.free_shipping} of={data.parcels_considered} hasOrderValues={hasOrderValues} />}
      {mixedPrices && (
        <p className="verdict__note">
          You charge {formatUsd(charged)} per parcel on average: the flat {formatUsd(controls.flat_price)}, your own
          price on rows that have one{data.free_shipping && data.free_shipping.parcels > 0 ? ", and $0 on free-shipping orders" : ""}.
        </p>
      )}
      <table className="ledger-table margin-table">
        <caption className="visually-hidden">Margin per parcel and share shipped at a loss, per carrier</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th scope="col">Carrier</th>
            <th scope="col" className="num">
              Cost / parcel
            </th>
            <th scope="col" className="num">
              Margin / parcel
            </th>
            <th scope="col">Shipped at a loss</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {[...rows.map((r) => ({ key: r.carrier as string, name: carrierName(r.carrier), color: carrierVar(r.carrier), s: r })),
            { key: "mix", name: "Best mix", color: "var(--ink)", s: data.best_mix }].map(({ key, name, color, s }) => (
            <tr key={key} data-testid={`margin-${key}`}>
              <th scope="row" style={{ color }}>
                {name}
              </th>
              <td className="num">{formatUsd(s.cost_per_parcel)}</td>
              <td className={`num ${marginClass(s.margin_per_parcel)}`}>{marginText(s.margin_per_parcel)}</td>
              <td>
                <span className="meter" aria-hidden="true">
                  <span className="meter__fill" style={{ transform: `scaleX(${s.loss_share})` }} />
                </span>
                <span className="meter__text">{formatShare(s.loss_share)}</span>
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </section>
```

```
  it("puts the cheapest carrier's cost per parcel first, and says when the best mix saves nothing", async () => {
    const { client, blended } = spyClient();
    renderWith(client, <BlendedView controls={controls} meta={meta} parcels={sampleParcels} {...parcelActions} />);
    expect(await screen.findByTestId("q3-answer")).toHaveTextContent(/^USPS \$10\.98 per parcel$/);
    expect(screen.getByText(/Blended over every parcel/)).toHaveTextContent("UPS $31.14 (+$20.16), FedEx $31.22 (+$20.24).");
    expect(blended).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ parcels: sampleParcels, controls }), expect.anything());
    expect(blended.mock.calls[0]![0]).not.toHaveProperty("segment");
    const mix = screen.getByTestId("q3-mix");
    expect(mix).toHaveTextContent("$10.98");
    expect(mix).toHaveTextContent("no saving over USPS alone.");
    expect(mix).not.toHaveTextContent("less per parcel");
    expect(mix).toHaveTextContent(/^.*USPS 307$/);

    const mix = await screen.findByTestId("q3-mix");

    expect(mix).toHaveTextContent("USPS 280 · FedEx 17 · UPS 10");
```

I've fixed six of the seven #27 items, plus a heatmap bug I found along the way. The seventh, the tab letter spacing, doesn't reproduce. It's all committed in four commits on branch `fix/ux-followups-27`, in the worktree `../parcel-rates-ux-27`. Nothing is pushed yet.

1. **Q4 dimming on open.** Opening Q4 sent two requests. The backend now applies the default next-year increase itself when the request leaves it out, so Q4 opens with one request. The response reports that default in a new field, `next_year_default_percent`.
   - This changes the API: a request without a percentage now projects every carrier, using the cheapest carrier's latest announced increase. Before, carriers with no announcement of their own got no projection.
   - I updated `docs/API.md`, the example payloads and the fixtures.
2. **Q4 driver bar colour.** "Cost up" bars are now neutral grey instead of the ochre that read as UPS brown. The grey and the teal "cost down" bars differ in lightness, not only hue, and both have at least 3:1 contrast with the background.
3. **Q2 near-ties.** When UPS and FedEx are within $0.10 of each other, the cell gets UPS/FedEx stripes, a "Tie" label, a legend entry and a tooltip note. USPS cells are never merged, so the line where USPS stops winning stays exact. Only 32 of the 94 non-USPS cells are within $0.10, so some thin UPS or FedEx rows remain; a higher threshold would merge more.
4. **Phone heatmap labels.** Carrier labels on phones are smaller, and any label that would touch its column edge is left out.
5. **Q3 slivers.** On the sample, additional handling, delivery area and extended delivery area now share one "Other surcharges" segment. The table still lists every kind.
6. **Q3 best mix.** When one carrier wins every parcel, the best mix is a single line ("USPS wins every parcel, so choosing per parcel saves nothing") and the duplicate table row is gone.
7. **Phone tab letter spacing.** I made no change. In a fresh render the tab spacing is normal, so the wide spacing came from the environment that captured the old screenshot.

**The bug:** the legend says a dotted line marks where UPS and FedEx swap, but that style was deleted in `a1842f5`. Since then those edges were drawn as heavy as the USPS line. I restored the dotted line; you can see it in my screenshots.

**Tests:** frontend (217 tests), backend, typecheck, lint and the production build all pass. I checked the views in mock-mode screenshots on desktop and phone, light and dark. I haven't run the Playwright end-to-end suite, which needs `docker compose up`. Because of that, the README images and `frontend/e2e/screenshots/` still show the old design.

Should I push and open a PR? I can also bring up the Docker stack first, run the end-to-end suite and regenerate the screenshots as part of the same PR.

```
export const tab = (page: Page, view: ViewId): Locator => page.locator(`#tab-${view}`);
export const panel = (page: Page): Locator => page.getByRole("tabpanel");

/** Switch to a question and wait for its API answer. */
export async function showView(page: Page, view: ViewId): Promise<void> {
  if (view === "quote") {
    await tab(page, view).click();
    await expect(page.getByTestId("quote-answer")).toBeVisible();
    return;
  }
  // Q4 asks twice: once with the controls, then again with the next-year increase its first
  // answer suggests. Wait for the second, or the view is still dimmed as "stale".
  const answer =
    view === "yearly"
      ? page.waitForResponse(
          (r) => new URL(r.url()).pathname === "/api/yearly" && "next_year_increase_percent" in (r.request().postDataJSON() ?? {}),
        )
      : nextResponse(page, `/api/${view}`);
  await tab(page, view).click();
  expect((await answer).ok()).toBe(true);
  const id = { breakeven: "breakeven-answer", blended: "q3-answer", yearly: "q4-answer" }[view];
  await expect(page.getByTestId(id)).toBeVisible();
}
```

```
from PIL import Image
```
````
src='frontend/e2e/screenshots/'; dst='docs/screenshots/'
def footer_top(im):
    g=im.convert('L'); w,h=g.size; px=g.load()
    for y in range(h-1, h//3, -1):
        dark=sum(1 for x in range(0,w,4) if px[x,y]<60)
        if dark > 0.6*(w/4): return y
    return h
def save(im,name):
    im.convert('RGB').quantize(256, method=Image.Quantize.MEDIANCUT, dither=Image.Dither.NONE).save(dst+name, optimize=True)
for s,d in [('q1-typical','q1-quote'),('q2','q2-breakeven'),('q3','q3-blended'),('q4','q4-yearly')]:
    im=Image.open(src+f'{s}-desktop-light.png'); y=footer_top(im)

for s,d in [('q1-typical-mobile-light','q1-quote-mobile'),('q3-mobile-dark','q3-blended-mobile-dark')]:

EOF
````

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Restore raw source files listed in data/raw/manifest.csv, politely.

Fetches one file at a time, pauses between requests, retries with backoff on
429/5xx/timeouts, identifies itself with a User-Agent, verifies SHA-256
checksums, and never re-downloads a file whose checksum already matches.

Usage:
  python3 data/fetch_raw.py                 # fetch everything missing or mismatched
  python3 data/fetch_raw.py --carrier ups   # one carrier
  python3 data/fetch_raw.py --verify        # only check what is on disk
  python3 data/fetch_raw.py --record        # fill empty sha256/bytes in the manifest after fetching

Standard library only, so it runs with any Python 3.8+.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import gzip
import hashlib
import random
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

RAW_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "raw"
MANIFEST = RAW_DIR / "manifest.csv"
USER_AGENT = (
    "Mozilla/5.0 (X11; Linux x86_64) parcel-rates-dashboard/0.1 "
    "(+https://github.com/aivuk/parcel-rates; polite sequential fetcher)"
)
PAUSE_SECONDS = 3.0
JITTER_SECONDS = 2.0
BACKOFF_SECONDS = (10, 30, 90)
TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 120
GZIP_MAGIC = b"\x1f\x8b"


def sha256_of(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1 << 20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def read_manifest() -> list[dict]:
    with MANIFEST.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        return list(csv.DictReader(handle))


def write_manifest(rows: list[dict]) -> None:
    fields = list(rows[0].keys())
    tmp = MANIFEST.with_suffix(".csv.tmp")
    with tmp.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=fields)
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)
    tmp.replace(MANIFEST)


def fetch_bytes(url: str) -> bytes:
    request = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": USER_AGENT})
    last_error: Exception | None = None
    for attempt, backoff in enumerate((*BACKOFF_SECONDS, None)):
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=TIMEOUT_SECONDS) as response:
                return response.read()
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
            last_error = error
            retryable = error.code == 429 or error.code >= 500
            if not retryable or backoff is None:
                raise
        except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, ConnectionError) as error:
            last_error = error
            if backoff is None:
                raise
        print(f"    retry {attempt + 1} in {backoff}s ({last_error})", flush=True)
        time.sleep(backoff)
    raise RuntimeError(f"unreachable: {last_error}")


def maybe_gunzip(data: bytes, target: Path) -> bytes:
    """Wayback's id_ passthrough returns the original bytes, which for some
    HTML captures are still gzip-encoded. Decompress unless the target is a
    gzip file by name."""
    if data[:2] == GZIP_MAGIC and target.suffix not in {".gz", ".zip"}:
        return gzip.decompress(data)
    return data


def status_of(row: dict) -> str:
    target = RAW_DIR / row["path"]
    if not target.is_file():
        return "missing"
    if not row["sha256"]:
        return "unverified"
    return "ok" if sha256_of(target) == row["sha256"] else "mismatch"


def fetch_row(row: dict) -> None:
    target = RAW_DIR / row["path"]
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    data = maybe_gunzip(fetch_bytes(row["url"]), target)
    tmp = target.with_name(target.name + ".part")
    tmp.write_bytes(data)
    tmp.replace(target)


def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.split("\n")[0])
    parser.add_argument("--carrier", help="only this carrier")
    parser.add_argument("--verify", action="store_true", help="check only, fetch nothing")
    parser.add_argument("--record", action="store_true", help="fill empty sha256/bytes after fetching")
    parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=0, help="stop after N fetches")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    rows = read_manifest()
    selected = [r for r in rows if not args.carrier or r["carrier"] == args.carrier]
    fetched = failed = 0
    for row in selected:
        state = status_of(row)
        target = RAW_DIR / row["path"]
        if state == "ok":
            continue
        if state == "unverified" and not args.record:
            print(f"?? {row['path']}: present, no checksum in manifest")
            continue
        if args.verify:
            print(f"!! {row['path']}: {state}")
            failed += 1
            continue
        if state in {"missing", "mismatch"}:
            if fetched:
                time.sleep(PAUSE_SECONDS + random.uniform(0, JITTER_SECONDS))
            print(f".. {row['path']} <- {row['url']}", flush=True)
            try:
                fetch_row(row)
                fetched += 1
            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - report and continue with the next file
                print(f"!! {row['path']}: {error}")
                failed += 1
                continue
        if args.record and target.is_file():
            digest = sha256_of(target)
            if row["sha256"] and row["sha256"] != digest:
                print(f"!! {row['path']}: checksum changed, not overwriting manifest")
                failed += 1
                continue
            row["sha256"] = digest
            row["bytes"] = str(target.stat().st_size)
        if args.limit and fetched >= args.limit:
            break
    if args.record:
        write_manifest(rows)
    print(f"done: {fetched} fetched, {failed} problems, {len(selected)} rows considered")
    return 1 if failed else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))
"""Find the carriers' announced increases for a coming year, by rule, with no agent involved.

Issue #11: the year-over-year view projects each carrier's next year from its own announced
general rate increase. Announcements arrive months before the price lists (UPS and FedEx in
September to November, USPS in October to November), so this script watches the pages where
they appear, keeps a copy of each source in data/raw (with a manifest row and checksum) and
records the percentage with a verbatim quote in data/collect/announcements_found.csv. The
carrier pipelines merge those rows into data/processed/<carrier>/announced_increase.csv, and
data/tests/test_citations.py checks every quote against the saved page.

Sources, per carrier (each fetched live first, then from the latest Wayback capture):

- USPS (primary): the newsroom release that announces the January prices for the year,
  found on the national-releases index or by a Wayback CDX listing of the previous year's
  releases; the percentages per product come from its text.
- UPS (secondary): UPS's own rate pages render in the browser and carry no text, so the
  dated rate-increase table on ebb Logistics' GRI history page (already the source of the
  UPS 2024 row) is read: "<year> <percent>% <effective date>" in the UPS column.
- FedEx (primary): the FedEx rate-changes page, "will increase an average of <percent>%"
  under an "Effective <date>" heading in the target year.

Usage (polite: sequential, pauses with jitter, retries with backoff, identifying User-Agent):

    uv run python -m data.collect.announcements                 # next calendar year
    uv run python -m data.collect.announcements --year 2027
    uv run python -m data.collect.announcements --check         # report only, write nothing

Then rebuild the tables: uv run python -m data.pipeline.<carrier> for each carrier that changed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import gzip
import hashlib
import html
import json
import random
import re
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path

DATA = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
RAW = DATA / "raw"
MANIFEST = RAW / "manifest.csv"
FOUND = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "announcements_found.csv"
FOUND_COLUMNS = [
    "carrier", "year", "product_scope", "rate_card", "percent", "source_type",
    "source_file", "source_ref", "source_quote", "url", "found_on",
]

USER_AGENT = (
    "Mozilla/5.0 (X11; Linux x86_64) parcel-rates-dashboard/0.1 "
    "(+https://github.com/aivuk/parcel-rates; polite announcement watcher)"
```

```
[project]
name = "parcel-rates-data"
version = "0.1.0"
description = "Data collection and rate-table pipeline for the parcel-rates dashboard"
requires-python = ">=3.12"
dependencies = [
    "pdfplumber>=0.11",
    "openpyxl>=3.1",

[dependency-groups]

    "pytest>=8",
]

[tool.uv]
package = false

[tool.pytest.ini_options]
testpaths = ["data/tests"]
addopts = "-ra"

# One lint config for data/ and backend/ (backend/pyproject.toml has no [tool.ruff],
# so ruff uses this one). Run with `uvx ruff check backend data`.
[tool.ruff]
target-version = "py312"
line-length = 120

[tool.ruff.lint]
select = ["E4", "E7", "E9", "F", "I", "B"]
# zip(strict=True) would change behaviour on uneven inputs; reviewed case by case instead.
ignore = ["B905"]

[tool.ruff.lint.flake8-bugbear]
extend-immutable-calls = ["fastapi.Depends", "fastapi.Body", "fastapi.Query"]

[tool.ruff.lint.isort]
known-first-party = ["app", "data"]
```

```
# USPS raw sources — data-availability survey (retrieved 2026-10-22)

Carrier: USPS, domestic **retail** (published, non-contract) list prices.
Scope asked: 2022–2026, services Ground Advantage (since 2023-07-09; before that First-Class Package
Service / Parcel Select Ground / USPS Retail Ground), Priority Mail, Priority Mail Express, zones 1–9.

All downloads were made directly from the live USPS sites with `curl -A "Mozilla/5.0 ..."`.
Note: pe.usps.com returns HTTP 404 to clients without a browser-like User-Agent and to HTTP
range requests; with a UA every file below came back 200. The Wayback Machine CDX index
(`web.archive.org/cdx/search/cdx?url=pe.usps.com/resources/PriceChange/*`) was used only to
*discover* the exact file names of older price-change zips; the files themselves were then
fetched from pe.usps.com directly (not from Wayback).

Price-change calendar covered (effective dates, from the PDFs' cover pages / DMM archive folder dates):
2022-01-09, 2022-07-10, 2022-10-02 (peak), 2023-01-22, 2023-07-09 (Ground Advantage launch),
2024-01-21, 2024-07-14, 2024-10-06 (peak), 2025-01-19, 2025-07-13, 2025-10-05 (peak),
2026-01-18, 2026-04-26 (transportation-related time-limited), 2026-07-12, 2026-10-04 (peak).
There was **no** USPS peak price change in autumn 2023 (see press release 2023-09-19 below).

## A. Notice 123 "Price List" PDFs (one per price change)

Source pattern: `https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive<YYYYMMDD>/Notice123.pdf` (DMM archive,
folder date = effective date), except the current one (`https://pe.usps.com/cpim/ftp/manuals/dmm300/notice123.pdf`)
and the April 2026 one (`https://pe.usps.com/resources/PriceChange/Transportation-Related%20Time-Limited%202026%20Price%20Change%20-%20Notice123%20PDF.pdf`).
Method: direct download. Format: PDF, 62–76 pages, text-based (not scanned).

Contents (all of them): domestic retail and commercial price tables for Priority Mail Express,
Priority Mail, Ground Advantage (2023-07 onward) / First-Class Package Service + USPS Retail Ground +
Parcel Select Ground (2022 and 2023-01), by zone 1–9 (zone 9 = Hawaii/Alaska-type) and weight
(Priority Mail / PME: 1–70 lb by pound; Ground Advantage retail: ounces up to 15.999 oz then pounds to 70 lb;
FCPS 2022: ounces to 15.999 oz), flat-rate prices, extra services, fees. Footnotes under each table
carry the surcharge rules (see section D).

Parse difficulty: PDF tables with footnotes printed in a right-hand column *beside* the table
(pdftotext -layout interleaves footnote text with price rows); tables for one product span
one or two pages. pdfplumber/camelot should work but the CSV zips (section B) are far easier —
use the PDFs for the footnote rules and as a cross-check only.

| File | URL | Effective |
|---|---|---|
| 2022/notice123_2022-01-09.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive20220109/Notice123.pdf | 2022-01-09 |
| 2022/notice123_2022-07-10.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive20220710/Notice123.pdf | 2022-07-10 |
| 2022/notice123_2022-10-02_peak.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive20221002/Notice123.pdf | 2022-10-02 to 2023-01-22 (peak) |
| 2023/notice123_2023-01-22.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive20230122/Notice123.pdf | 2023-01-22 |
| 2023/notice123_2023-07-09.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive20230709/Notice123.pdf | 2023-07-09 (first Ground Advantage prices) |
| 2024/notice123_2024-01-21.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive20240121/Notice123.pdf | 2024-01-21 |
| 2024/notice123_2024-07-14.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive20240714/Notice123.pdf | 2024-07-14 |
| 2024/notice123_2024-10-06_peak.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive20241006/Notice123.pdf | 2024-10-06 to 2025-01-19 (peak) |
| 2025/notice123_2025-01-19.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive20250119/Notice123.pdf | 2025-01-19 |
| 2025/notice123_2025-07-13.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive20250713/Notice123.pdf | 2025-07-13 |
| 2025/notice123_2025-10-05_peak.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive20251005/Notice123.pdf | 2025-10-05 to 2026-01-18 (peak) |
| 2026/notice123_2026-01-18.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive20260118/Notice123.pdf | 2026-01-18 |
| 2026/notice123_2026-04-26_transportation.pdf | (resources/PriceChange URL above) | 2026-04-26 to 2027-01-17 (time-limited +8 %) |
| 2026/notice123_2026-07-12.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive20260712/Notice123.pdf | 2026-07-12 |
| 2026/notice123_2026-10-04_peak.pdf | https://pe.usps.com/cpim/ftp/manuals/dmm300/notice123.pdf (current) | 2026-10-04 to 2027-01-17 (peak) |

## B. Price-file CSV zips (Postal Explorer "Price Files – CSV" / "Notice 123.zip")

Method: direct download from `https://pe.usps.com/resources/PriceChange/<name>` (names below).
Format: ZIP of ~50 CSV files (one per product). Relevant retail files inside each zip:
`PM Retail.csv`, `PME Retail.csv`, and `USPS Ground Advantage Retail.csv` (2023-07 onward) /
`FCPS.csv`, `USPS Retail Ground.csv`, `Parcel Select Ground.csv` (2022-01 … 2023-01).
Also `PM Comm.csv`, `PME Comm.csv`, `USPS Ground Advantage Comm.csv` (commercial, out of scope).

Layout (checked on 2026-10-04 zip): a 1–2 line title row (`Priority Mail - Retail,,Final,,9/14/2026`),
flat-rate rows, then a header `Weight Not Over (Lbs),Zones` / `,Zone 1,…,Zone 9` followed by one row
per weight step (ounces for Ground Advantage: 4, 8, 12, 15.999; then pounds 1–70). ~90 lines per file.
Parse difficulty: **clean CSV, but with irregular preamble rows** — locate the `Zone 1` header row
programmatically, then read the numeric block; a float like `15.999000000000001` appears for the
15.999-oz step. Surcharge footnotes are NOT in the CSVs (they are only in the PDF).

## C. Announced average price increases (USPS national press releases, PDF versions)

Method: direct download from `https://about.usps.com/newsroom/national-releases/<yyyy>/<slug>.pdf`.
Format: 1–2 page text PDF, trivial to parse. Percentages quoted below were extracted with pdftotext
from the saved file named in each row.

| File | Covers | Quoted figures (from the file) |
|---|---|---|
| 2022/press_2021-11-10_jan2022_competitive_prices.pdf | 2022-01-09 | "approximately 3.1 percent for Priority Mail service, and 3.1 percent for Priority Mail Express" (First-Class Package Service figure not in the parsed lines; check file) |
| 2022/press_2022-04-06_jul2022_prices.pdf | 2022-07-10 | Market-dominant release: "First-Class Mail prices approximately 6.5 percent". No shipping-services percent found in it; July 2022 competitive % **not verified**. |
| 2022/press_2022-08-10_peak2022_temporary_rates.pdf | 2022-10-02 → 2023-01-22 peak | dollar add-ons by product/zone/weight band (see file) |
| 2023/press_2022-11-10_jan2023_competitive_prices.pdf | 2023-01-22 | "Priority Mail … approximately 5.5 percent, Priority Mail Express … 6.6 percent, and First-Class Package Service … 7.8 percent" |
| 2023/press_2023-05-10_jul2023_ground_advantage.pdf | 2023-07-09 | Ground Advantage launch release (retail GA prices announced as a decrease vs FCPS/Retail Ground; exact % to be read from file) |
| 2023/press_2023-09-19_no_peak_surcharge_2023.pdf | autumn 2023 | "No holiday surcharges for mailing and shipping with USPS this holiday season" |
| 2024/press_2023-11-15_jan2024_competitive_prices.pdf | 2024-01-21 | "USPS Ground Advantage … 5.4 percent, Priority Mail … 5.7 percent, and Priority Mail Express … 5.9 percent" |
| 2024/press_2024-05-10_jul2024_parcel_select.pdf | 2024-07-14 | Parcel Select only; no retail PM/PME/GA change announced for July 2024 |
| 2024/press_2024-09-05_peak2024_temporary_rates.pdf | 2024-10-06 → 2025-01-19 peak | dollar add-ons by product/zone/weight band |
| 2025/press_2024-11-15_jan2025_competitive_prices.pdf | 2025-01-19 | "approximately 3.2 percent for Priority Mail service and Priority Mail Express service, 3.9 percent for USPS Ground Advantage and 9.2 percent for Parcel Select" |
| 2025/press_2025-05-09_jul2025_shipping_prices.pdf | 2025-07-13 | "approximately 6.3 percent for Priority Mail service, 7.1 percent for USPS Ground Advantage and 7.6 percent for Parcel Select" |
| 2025/press_2025-08-08_peak2025_temporary_rates.pdf | 2025-10-05 → 2026-01-18 peak | dollar add-ons by product/zone/weight band |
| 2026/press_2025-11-14_jan2026_competitive_prices.pdf | 2026-01-18 | "approximately 6.6 percent for Priority Mail service, 5.1 percent for Priority Mail Express service, 7.8 percent for USPS Ground Advantage and 6.0 percent for Parcel Select" |
| 2026/press_2026-03-25_apr2026_transportation_time_limited.pdf | 2026-04-26 → 2027-01-17 | transportation-related time-limited increase on PME/PM/GA/Parcel Select (the 8 % figure comes from web search summaries; confirm in file) |
| 2026/press_2026-05-11_jul2026_competitive_price_changes.pdf | 2026-07-12 | no base-rate change for retail PM/PME/GA; "align the divisor for dimensional weight pieces to industry standards"; PO Box +3 % |
| 2026/press_2026-08-25_peak2026_temporary_rates.pdf | 2026-10-04 → 2027-01-17 peak | dollar add-ons by product/zone/weight band |

## D. Surcharge rules (what the fetched documents say)

From the Notice 123 PDFs (footnotes beside the Priority Mail / PME / Ground Advantage tables):
- Nonstandard length fees: "Parcels that exceed 22 inches but not greater than 30 inches in length, add $…" and
  "Parcels that exceed 30 inches in length, add $…". Over-30-in amounts seen: $15.00 (2022-01, "Effective April 3, 2022"), $15.00 (2023-07),
  $18.00 (2025-01), $21.00 (2026-07 and 2026-10). Ground Advantage over-30-in: $7.00 (2023-07), $8.40 (2025-01), $10.00 (2026).
- Volume fee: "Parcels that exceed 2 cubic feet (3456 cubic inches), add $…": $15.00 (2022), $25.00 (2023-07 PM/PME),
  $35.00 (2026 PM/PME), $21.00 (2026 Ground Advantage).
  The 22–30 in amounts are in the same footnotes; read them per year from the PDF (not extracted here).
- Dimensional weight: every year's footnote says "Parcels that exceed one cubic foot (1,728 cubic inches) are charged based on
  the actual weight or the dimensional weight, whichever is greater". The divisor and the zone scope are NOT stated in
  Notice 123 (it refers to DMM 123/223). Web summaries say divisor 166 through 2026-07-11 and 139 from 2026-07-12 for
  PME/PM/GA/Parcel Select (the 2026-05-11 press release confirms a divisor change "to industry standards" but does not
  print the number) — **divisor values and zone applicability (zones 1–4 vs 5–9 in earlier years) are unverified from
  saved files; source DMM 123.1.6 / 223.1.6 for the year in question**.
- Residential / delivery-area surcharges: none found in any Notice 123 or press release; the 2023-09-19 release explicitly
  says no extra fees for residential delivery or Saturday delivery. USPS has no such surcharge for retail parcels (consistent
  with all fetched docs; absence-of-evidence only).
- Peak season: temporary price changes replace the base tables (prices in the "_peak" PDFs/zips are the full tables, not an
  add-on). Windows: 2022-10-02→2023-01-22; none in 2023; 2024-10-06→2025-01-19; 2025-10-05→2026-01-18; 2026-10-04→2027-01-17.
  2026 additionally has the 2026-04-26→2027-01-17 transportation-related increase stacked under the peak prices.

## E. Zone chart

- zones/postalpro_zone_charts_page.html — https://postalpro.usps.com/operations/zone-charts (scraped HTML, 2026-10-22).
  Links to: Domestic Zone Chart tool, "Zone Charts Matrix Technical Guide" (/national-zone-charts-matrix/ZoneChartsMatrixTechnicalGuide,
  effective June 2024 — the link returned an HTML page, not a PDF, so it was not kept), "Zone Charts Order Form" (/Zone_Charts_Order_Form,
  dated 2026-07-12), Zone 10 test files. The full National Zone Charts Matrix (origin ZIP3 x destination ZIP3 → zone) is
  obtained via the order form, not as an open bulk download on that page.
- zones/postcalc_domestic_zone_chart_page.html — https://postcalc.usps.com/DomesticZoneChart (scraped HTML). Form-driven tool:
  given an origin ZIP3 it returns the zone for every destination ZIP3 range (with "same NDC" and 5-digit exceptions), optionally as
  an Excel-formatted table; endpoints seen in the page: `/DomesticZoneChart/GetZoneChart` and `/DomesticZoneChart/GetZone`
  (POST form, chart date selectable). That is the practical free source: ~1,000 ZIP3 requests build the full matrix.
  Zones are distance-based (DMM 608.9 ranges; zone 9 = non-contiguous/territories); not verified here beyond the page text.

## E. Domestic Mail Manual sections (dimensional weight rule), added 2026-10-22

Notice 123 does not print the dimensional-weight divisor; DMM 123 (Priority Mail) and
DMM 133 (Ground Advantage) do. Saved under `dmm/`:

| File | URL | Method | Says |
|---|---|---|---|
| dmm/dmm123_2024-03.htm | https://web.archive.org/web/20240304155059id_/https://pe.usps.com/text/dmm300/123.htm | Wayback (gunzipped) | "If the result exceeds 1,728 cubic inches, divide the result by 166" |
| dmm/dmm133_2024-03.htm | https://web.archive.org/web/20240225004436id_/https://pe.usps.com/text/dmm300/133.htm | Wayback (gunzipped) | same, divisor 166 |
| dmm/dmm123_2026-10-22.htm | https://pe.usps.com/text/dmm300/123.htm | direct, browser User-Agent | "[7-12-26] … divide the result by 139" |
| dmm/dmm133_2026-10-22.htm | https://pe.usps.com/text/dmm300/133.htm | direct, browser User-Agent | same, divisor 139 |

Neither version restricts the rule by zone. Press-release slugs and CSV-zip URLs
for every file are in `../manifest.csv`.
```

```
def fetch(url: str) -> bytes | None:
    """GET with retries; None when the resource does not exist or keeps failing."""
    for attempt, wait in enumerate((0, *BACKOFF_SECONDS)):
        if wait:
            time.sleep(wait)
        time.sleep(PAUSE_SECONDS + random.uniform(0, JITTER_SECONDS))
        request = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": USER_AGENT})
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=TIMEOUT_SECONDS) as response:
                body = response.read()
            # Wayback id_ captures keep the original Content-Encoding, often gzip.
            return gzip.decompress(body) if body[:2] == b"\x1f\x8b" else body
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as err:
            if err.code not in (429, 500, 502, 503, 504):
                print(f"  {err.code} {url}")
                return None
            print(f"  {err.code} {url}, retry {attempt + 1}")
        except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as err:
            print(f"  {err} {url}, retry {attempt + 1}")
    return None


def latest_capture(url: str) -> str | None:
    """The id_ passthrough URL of the latest 200 capture of `url` in the Wayback Machine."""
    query = f"{CDX}?url={urllib.request.quote(url, safe='')}&output=json&fl=timestamp,original&filter=statuscode:200&limit=-1"
    body = fetch(query)
    if not body:
        return None
    rows = json.loads(body.decode("utf-8") or "[]")[1:]
    if not rows:
        return None
    timestamp, original = rows[-1]
    return f"https://web.archive.org/web/{timestamp}id_/{original}"


def cdx_prefix(prefix: str) -> list[str]:
    """Original URLs of 200 captures under a URL prefix (one per URL)."""
    query = f"{CDX}?url={urllib.request.quote(prefix, safe='')}&matchType=prefix&output=json&fl=original&filter=statuscode:200&collapse=urlkey"
    body = fetch(query)
    if not body:
        return []
    return [row[0] for row in json.loads(body.decode("utf-8") or "[]")[1:]]


BLOCK_PAGE = re.compile(r"(?i)you don't have permission to view|access denied|request unsuccessful|captcha")


def blocked(body: bytes) -> bool:
    """A bot-protection page served with status 200 (FedEx does this)."""
    return bool(BLOCK_PAGE.search(page_text(body[:20000].decode("utf-8", "replace"))))


def get_page(url: str) -> tuple[str, bytes] | None:
    """(where it came from, body): live first, else the latest Wayback capture."""
    body = fetch(url)
    if body and not blocked(body):
        return url, body
    if body:
        print(f"  blocked live page, trying the Wayback Machine: {url}")
    capture = latest_capture(url)
    if capture:
        body = fetch(capture)
        if body:
            return capture, body
    return None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- per carrier
def find_usps(year: int) -> list[tuple[Found, str, bytes]]:
    links: list[str] = []
    index = get_page(USPS_INDEX)
    "(+https://github.com/aivuk/parcel-rates; polite announcement watcher)"
)
PAUSE_SECONDS = 3.0
JITTER_SECONDS = 2.0
BACKOFF_SECONDS = (10, 30, 90)
TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 90

USPS_INDEX = "https://about.usps.com/newsroom/national-releases/"
USPS_RELEASES = "about.usps.com/newsroom/national-releases/{year}/"
UPS_TABLE = "https://ebblogistics.com/ups-fedex-rate-increase-history/"
FEDEX_PAGE = "https://www.fedex.com/en-us/shipping/rate-changes.html"
CDX = "https://web.archive.org/cdx/search/cdx"

MONTH = r"(?:January|February|March|April|May|June|July|August|September|October|November|December)"
NUMBER = r"(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)"
SHORT_MONTH = r"(?:Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|June?|July?|Aug|Sept?|Oct|Nov|Dec)[a-z]*\.?"

# USPS product names as the releases write them -> product_scope in announced_increase.csv.
# "Priority Mail" must not match the start of "Priority Mail Express".
USPS_PRODUCTS = [
    ("usps_ground_advantage", r"USPS Ground Advantage"),
    ("usps_priority_mail_express", r"Priority Mail Express"),
    ("usps_priority_mail", r"Priority Mail(?! Express)"),
]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Found:
    carrier: str
    year: int
    product_scope: str
    rate_card: str
    percent: str
    source_type: str
    quote: str
```

I'm writing the Wayback CDX client now. It has a live mode that can record every response, and a replay mode that reads the recording, so the tests run offline:

**Tool call:** `Write` <sub>toolu_017C1QqKLFbZG7uAyA4StdzJ</sub>

- `file_path`: `~/parcel-rates-issue-13/data/collect/wayback.py`
- `content`:

```
"""Wayback Machine CDX lookups and capture selection, for source discovery (issue #13).

The carriers' own sites block scripted clients or replace files in place, so most raw
sources are taken from the Wayback Machine. Which capture to take is decided here by
rule, never by hand: query the CDX index for a URL (or a URL prefix), then pick the
capture a `Pick` describes. The id_ passthrough URL of that capture returns the
original bytes.

Two clients answer CDX queries:

- `LiveCdx` asks web.archive.org, politely (sequential, a pause with jitter between
  requests, backoff on 429 and 5xx, an identifying User-Agent). It can keep every
  response it got, so a run can be recorded.
- `RecordedCdx` answers from such a recording, so tests and `--offline` runs need no
  network. data/tests/fixtures/cdx.json.gz is the recording the tests use.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import gzip
import json
import random
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Protocol

CDX = "https://web.archive.org/cdx/search/cdx"
FIELDS = "urlkey,timestamp,original,statuscode,mimetype,digest"
USER_AGENT = (
    "Mozilla/5.0 (X11; Linux x86_64) parcel-rates-dashboard/0.1 "
    "(+https://github.com/aivuk/parcel-rates; polite source discovery)"
)
PAUSE_SECONDS = 3.0
JITTER_SECONDS = 2.0
BACKOFF_SECONDS = (10, 30, 90)
TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 180


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Capture:
    urlkey: str
    timestamp: str  # YYYYMMDDhhmmss
    original: str
    status: str  # "200", "301", ...; "-" for a revisit record (same bytes as an earlier capture)
    mimetype: str
    digest: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Pick:
    """Which capture to take: the latest (or earliest) good capture in [after, before).

    `after` and `before` are dates (YYYY-MM-DD) or 14-digit timestamps; either may be
    empty. A good capture has status 200, or is a revisit of bytes some 200 capture
    returned. `mimetype`, when set, must be contained in the capture's type.
    """

    before: str = ""
    after: str = ""
    latest: bool = True
    mimetype: str = ""


def stamp(value: str) -> str:
    """A date or timestamp as a 14-digit CDX timestamp; '' stays ''."""
    digits = value.replace("-", "")
    return digits.ljust(14, "0") if digits else ""


def good(captures: list[Capture], mimetype: str = "") -> list[Capture]:
    ok_digests = {c.digest for c in captures if c.status == "200"}
    keep = [c for c in captures if c.status == "200" or (c.status == "-" and c.digest in ok_digests)]
    return [c for c in keep if mimetype in c.mimetype] if mimetype else keep


def select(captures: list[Capture], pick: Pick) -> Capture | None:
    lo, hi = stamp(pick.after), stamp(pick.before)
    window = [c for c in good(captures, pick.mimetype) if (not lo or c.timestamp >= lo) and (not hi or c.timestamp < hi)]
    if not window:
        return None
    window.sort(key=lambda c: c.timestamp)
    return window[-1] if pick.latest else window[0]


def id_url(timestamp: str, original: str) -> str:
    """The passthrough URL that returns the capture's original bytes."""
    return f"https://web.archive.org/web/{timestamp}id_/{original}"


def query_string(url: str, prefix: bool = False) -> str:
    params = {"url": url, "output": "json", "fl": FIELDS}
    if prefix:
        params |= {"matchType": "prefix", "collapse": "urlkey"}
    return urllib.parse.urlencode(params)


def parse(body: str) -> list[Capture]:
    rows = json.loads(body or "[]")
    return [Capture(*row) for row in rows[1:]]


class CdxClient(Protocol):
    def raw(self, query: str) -> str: ...


def captures(client: CdxClient, url: str) -> list[Capture]:
    """Every capture of one URL."""
    return parse(client.raw(query_string(url)))


def listing(client: CdxClient, prefix: str) -> list[Capture]:
    """The first capture of every URL under a prefix."""
    return parse(client.raw(query_string(prefix, prefix=True)))


class LiveCdx:
    def __init__(self, keep: bool = False) -> None:
        self.kept: dict[str, str] = {}
        self.keep = keep
        self._last = 0.0

    def raw(self, query: str) -> str:
        if query in self.kept:
            return self.kept[query]
        body = self._get(f"{CDX}?{query}")
        if self.keep:
            self.kept[query] = body
        return body

    def _get(self, url: str) -> str:
        request = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": USER_AGENT})
        for attempt, backoff in enumerate((*BACKOFF_SECONDS, None)):
            wait = PAUSE_SECONDS + random.uniform(0, JITTER_SECONDS) - (time.monotonic() - self._last)
            if wait > 0:
                time.sleep(wait)
            try:
                with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=TIMEOUT_SECONDS) as response:
                    return response.read().decode("utf-8")
            except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
                if not (error.code == 429 or error.code >= 500) or backoff is None:
                    raise
                problem: Exception = error
            except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, ConnectionError) as error:
                if backoff is None:
                    raise
                problem = error
            finally:
                self._last = time.monotonic()
            print(f"    CDX retry {attempt + 1} in {backoff}s ({problem})", flush=True)
            time.sleep(backoff)
        raise RuntimeError("unreachable")

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with gzip.open(path, "wt", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            json.dump(dict(sorted(self.kept.items())), handle, indent=0)


class RecordedCdx:
    def __init__(self, path: Path) -> None:
        with gzip.open(path, "rt", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            self.responses: dict[str, str] = json.load(handle)

    def raw(self, query: str) -> str:
        if query not in self.responses:
            raise KeyError(f"no recorded CDX response for {urllib.parse.unquote(query)}")
        return self.responses[query]

```

```
s=open(p).read()
def rep(o,n):
    global s
    assert o in s, o[:40]; s=s.replace(o,n,1)
rep('''def query_string(url: str, prefix: bool = False) -> str:
    params = {"url": url, "output": "json", "fl": FIELDS}
    if prefix:
        params |= {"matchType": "prefix", "collapse": "urlkey"}
    return urllib.parse.urlencode(params)''','''def query_string(url: str, prefix: bool = False, pick: Pick | None = None) -> str:
    params = {"url": url, "output": "json", "fl": FIELDS}
    if prefix:
        params |= {"matchType": "prefix", "collapse": "urlkey"}
    # The window narrows the answer (CDX bounds are inclusive; select() applies the exact ones).
    if pick and pick.after:
        params["from"] = stamp(pick.after)
    if pick and pick.before:
        params["to"] = stamp(pick.before)
    return urllib.parse.urlencode(params)''')
rep('''def captures(client: CdxClient, url: str) -> list[Capture]:
    """Every capture of one URL."""
    return parse(client.raw(query_string(url)))''','''def captures(client: CdxClient, url: str, pick: Pick | None = None) -> list[Capture]:
    """Every capture of one URL, within the pick's window when one is given."""
    return parse(client.raw(query_string(url, pick=pick)))''')
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
```

- `command`:

```
S=/tmp/claude-1000/-home-aivuk-parcel-rates/d51a77f1-d518-4d20-9d39-1d1d92c6d714/scratchpad; cat > $S/gen_sources.py <<'EOF'
import csv, re, datetime, sys
rows = list(csv.DictReader(open('data/raw/manifest.csv')))
PREFIXES = {
    "https://www.fedex.com/content/dam/fedex/us-united-states/services/": "FEDEX_DOCS",
    "https://www.fedex.com/en-us/shipping/": "FEDEX_PAGES",
    "https://www.ups.com/assets/resources/webcontent/en_US/": "UPS_DOCS",
    "https://www.ups.com/media/us/currentrates/zone-csv/": "UPS_ZONES",
    "https://pe.usps.com/text/dmm300/": "USPS_DMM",
}
def lit(s): return repr(s)
def orig_expr(o):
    for p, name in PREFIXES.items():
        if o.startswith(p): return f"{name} + {lit(o[len(p):])}"
    return lit(o)
family = re.compile(r'^usps/\d{4}/(notice123|price_files_csv)_')
out = []
for r in rows:
    if family.match(r['path']): continue
    year = r['year'] or ''
    common = f"{lit(r['carrier'])}, {lit(year)}, {lit(r['path'])}"
    tail = f"in_git={r['in_git']=='true'}, content={lit(r['content'])}"
    if r['method'] == 'direct':
        out.append(f"    direct({common}, {lit(r['url'])}, {tail}),")
    else:
        m = re.match(r'https://web\.archive\.org/web/(\d{14})id_/(.*)$', r['url'])
        ts, o = m.groups()
        d = datetime.date(int(ts[:4]), int(ts[4:6]), int(ts[6:8])) + datetime.timedelta(days=1)
        exact = ts[8:] == '000000'
        before = f"{d.isoformat()}"
        mark = "  # FIXME nearest" if exact else ""
        out.append(f"    archived({common}, {orig_expr(o)}, before={lit(before)}, {tail}),{mark}")
print("\n".join(out))
EOF
cd ~/parcel-rates-issue-13 && python3 $S/gen_sources.py > $S/entries.txt; wc -l $S/entries.txt; grep -c FIXME $S/entries.txt; head -3 $S/entries.txt | cut -c1-250
```

**Tool call:** `Bash` <sub>toolu_01AjVdQBpMZNPm4TYUXrnRJR</sub>

- `command`:

```
S=/tmp/claude-1000/-home-aivuk-parcel-rates/d51a77f1-d518-4d20-9d39-1d1d92c6d714/scratchpad; cd ~/parcel-rates-issue-13 && python3 - <<EOF
entries=open('$S/entries.txt').read()
head='''"""The raw-source catalogue: every file in data/raw/manifest.csv, and the rule that finds it.

Discovery (data/collect/discover.py) turns these entries into manifest rows with no agent
involved. Three kinds of entry:

- \`direct\`: a URL on the publisher's own site that serves the file as is. Entries marked
  \`live\` point at a page that is replaced in place (a "current" price list, a DMM section),
  so a later fetch can return newer bytes; their copies are kept in git.
- \`archived\`: a file taken from the Wayback Machine. The entry gives the publisher's URL
  and a \`Pick\` (the latest good capture before a cut-off date); discovery asks the CDX
  index and emits the id_ passthrough URL of the capture the rule selects. The cut-offs
  reproduce the captures the data were built from; to take a newer version, move the
  cut-off and re-run discovery, then the fetch and the data tests.
- USPS price changes: one entry per price change in \`USPS_PRICE_CHANGES\` gives its
  Notice 123 PDF (the DMM archive folder named after the effective date) and its price-file
  zip, whose file name is discovered from the CDX listing of Postal Explorer's PriceChange
  folder (see \`usps_price_file\`).

Order matters: it is the order of the manifest rows.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import urllib.parse
from dataclasses import dataclass

from data.collect.wayback import Capture, Pick

FEDEX_DOCS = "https://www.fedex.com/content/dam/fedex/us-united-states/services/"
FEDEX_PAGES = "https://www.fedex.com/en-us/shipping/"
UPS_DOCS = "https://www.ups.com/assets/resources/webcontent/en_US/"
UPS_ZONES = "https://www.ups.com/media/us/currentrates/zone-csv/"
USPS_DMM = "https://pe.usps.com/text/dmm300/"
USPS_DMM_ARCHIVE = "https://pe.usps.com/Archive/PDF/DMMArchive{date}/Notice123.pdf"
USPS_PRICE_FOLDER = "pe.usps.com/resources/PriceChange/"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Source:
    carrier: str
    year: str
    path: str
    content: str
    in_git: bool
    url: str = ""  # direct: the file itself
    original: str = ""  # archived: the publisher's URL
    pick: Pick | None = None
    live: bool = False  # direct, and replaced in place by the publisher
    price_label: str = ""  # USPS price-file zip: the label its file name starts with

    @property
    def method(self) -> str:
        return "wayback" if self.original else "direct"


def direct(carrier: str, year: str, path: str, url: str, *, in_git: bool, content: str, live: bool = False) -> Source:
    return Source(carrier, year, path, content, in_git, url=url, live=live)


def archived(carrier: str, year: str, path: str, original: str, *, before: str, in_git: bool, content: str,
             after: str = "", mimetype: str = "") -> Source:
    return Source(carrier, year, path, content, in_git, original=original,
                  pick=Pick(before=before, after=after, mimetype=mimetype))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- USPS price changes
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PriceChange:
    effective: str  # YYYY-MM-DD, also the DMM archive folder date
    label: str  # how Postal Explorer names its files, e.g. "July 2024", "Time-Limited 2025"
    kind: str = ""  # "", "peak" or "transportation"
    ends: str = ""  # last day of a time-limited change
    note: str = ""
    notice_url: str = ""  # when the Notice 123 PDF is not in the DMM archive folder


USPS_PRICE_CHANGES = [
    PriceChange("2022-01-09", "January 2022", note="PM, PME, FCPS, Retail Ground, Parcel Select Ground tables by zone and weight; surcharge footnotes"),
    PriceChange("2022-07-10", "July 2022"),
    PriceChange("2022-10-02", "October 2022", "peak", "2023-01-22"),
    PriceChange("2023-01-22", "January 2023"),
    PriceChange("2023-07-09", "July 2023", note="first Ground Advantage prices"),
    PriceChange("2024-01-21", "January 2024"),
    PriceChange("2024-07-14", "July 2024"),
    PriceChange("2024-10-06", "October 2024", "peak", "2025-01-19"),
    PriceChange("2025-01-19", "January 2025"),
    PriceChange("2025-07-13", "July 2025"),
    PriceChange("2025-10-05", "Time-Limited 2025", "peak", "2026-01-18"),
    PriceChange("2026-01-18", "January 2026"),
    PriceChange("2026-04-26", "Transportation-Related Time-Limited 2026", "transportation", "2027-01-17",
                notice_url="https://pe.usps.com/resources/PriceChange/Transportation-Related%20Time-Limited%202026%20Price%20Change%20-%20Notice123%20PDF.pdf"),
    PriceChange("2026-07-12", "July 2026"),
    PriceChange("2026-10-04", "Time-Limited OCT 2026", "peak", "2027-01-17",
                notice_url="https://pe.usps.com/cpim/ftp/manuals/dmm300/notice123.pdf"),
]

SUFFIX = {"": "", "peak": "_peak", "transportation": "_transportation"}


def notice_source(pc: PriceChange) -> Source:
    year = pc.effective[:4]
    path = f"usps/{year}/notice123_{pc.effective}{SUFFIX[pc.kind]}.pdf"
    current = pc.notice_url.endswith("/dmm300/notice123.pdf")
    head = "Notice 123 Price List PDF" + (" (current)" if current else "")
    if pc.kind == "peak":
        content = f"{head}, peak-season prices {pc.effective} to {pc.ends}"
    elif pc.kind == "transportation":
        content = f"{head}, transportation-related time-limited prices {pc.effective} to {pc.ends}"
    else:
        content = f"{head} effective {pc.effective}" + (f" ({pc.note})" if pc.note else "")
    url = pc.notice_url or USPS_DMM_ARCHIVE.format(date=pc.effective.replace("-", ""))
    return direct("usps", year, path, url, in_git=False, content=content, live=current)


def zip_source(pc: PriceChange) -> Source:
    year = pc.effective[:4]
    note = {"": "", "peak": " (peak)", "transportation": " (transportation time-limited)"}[pc.kind]
    return Source(
        "usps", year, f"usps/{year}/price_files_csv_{pc.effective}{SUFFIX[pc.kind]}.zip",
        "Postal Explorer price-file CSV zip (~50 CSVs, one per product incl. PM/PME/GA retail) "
        f"effective {pc.effective}{note}",
        in_git=True, price_label=pc.label,
    )


# A price change's files are revised (draft, proposed, final) under new names. The zip with
# the retail CSVs is a "Notice 123" zip (2023 on) or a "Price Files - CSV" zip (before);
# SKU files, drafts, redlines, proposals and the alternative scenarios USPS published while
# a case was pending are not. Of the rest, the file the Wayback Machine first saw last is
# the final one.
PRICE_FILE_KINDS = re.compile(r"(Notice 123|Price Files - CSV)")
NOT_FINAL = re.compile(r"(?i)SKU|Draft|Redline|Proposed|HPM|BPM|Status Quo")


def usps_price_file(label: str, folder: list[Capture]) -> str | None:
    """The price-file zip of the price change named \`label\`, from a CDX listing of the folder."""
    best: tuple[str, str] | None = None
    for capture in folder:
        name = urllib.parse.unquote(capture.original.split("/PriceChange/", 1)[-1])
        if capture.status != "200" or not name.endswith(".zip") or not name.startswith(label + " "):
            continue
        if not PRICE_FILE_KINDS.search(name) or NOT_FINAL.search(name):
            continue
        if best is None or capture.timestamp > best[0]:
            best = (capture.timestamp, name)
    return f"https://{USPS_PRICE_FOLDER}{urllib.parse.quote(best[1])}" if best else None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- everything else
OTHER_SOURCES = [
'''
tail=''']

SOURCES: list[Source] = [
    *(notice_source(pc) for pc in USPS_PRICE_CHANGES),
    *(zip_source(pc) for pc in USPS_PRICE_CHANGES),
    *OTHER_SOURCES,
]
'''
open('data/collect/sources.py','w').write(head+entries+'\n'+tail)
EOF
wc -l data/collect/sources.py; python3 -c "
import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'.')
from data.collect.sources import SOURCES; print(len(SOURCES))"
```

**Tool call:** `Write` <sub>toolu_01Kwa24JSUHTMBERfqiRHGoG</sub>

- `content`:

```
"""Rebuild data/raw/manifest.csv from the source catalogue, with no agent in the loop (issue #13).

Every row comes from an entry in data/collect/sources.py: a direct URL, a USPS price-file
name found in the CDX listing of Postal Explorer's PriceChange folder, or the Wayback
capture a rule selects. Checksums are carried over from the committed manifest when a row's
URL is unchanged; a new or changed URL gets empty ones, which the fetch fills in.

    uv run python -m data.collect.discover                # compare with the manifest, write nothing
    uv run python -m data.collect.discover --write        # rewrite the manifest
    uv run python -m data.collect.discover --newer        # also list captures after each cut-off
    python3 data/fetch_raw.py --record                    # then fetch and fill in checksums

From a clean clone the two commands rebuild data/raw: discovery writes the manifest, the
fetcher downloads what is not in git and checks every file against its checksum.

`--offline FILE` answers CDX queries from a recording (data/tests/fixtures/cdx.json.gz is
the one the tests use) and `--record FILE` writes one. CDX lookups are sequential with a
3 to 5 s pause; a full run asks about 90 questions and takes ten minutes or more.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from data.collect import wayback
from data.collect.sources import SOURCES, USPS_PRICE_FOLDER, Source, usps_price_file

RAW = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "raw"
MANIFEST = RAW / "manifest.csv"
FIXTURE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "tests" / "fixtures" / "cdx.json.gz"
COLUMNS = ["carrier", "year", "path", "url", "method", "sha256", "bytes", "in_git", "content"]


class Unresolved(Exception):
    pass


class Discovery:
    def __init__(self, client: wayback.CdxClient) -> None:
        self.client = client
        self._folder: list[wayback.Capture] | None = None

    def price_folder(self) -> list[wayback.Capture]:
        if self._folder is None:
            self._folder = wayback.listing(self.client, USPS_PRICE_FOLDER)
        return self._folder

    def url(self, source: Source) -> str:
        if source.price_label:
            url = usps_price_file(source.price_label, self.price_folder())
            if not url:
                raise Unresolved(f"no price-file zip named {source.price_label!r} in the PriceChange listing")
            return url
        if source.original:
            capture = wayback.select(wayback.captures(self.client, source.original, source.pick), source.pick)
            if not capture:
                raise Unresolved(f"no capture of {source.original} matches {source.pick}")
            return wayback.id_url(capture.timestamp, source.original)
        return source.url

    def newer(self, source: Source, chosen: str) -> list[wayback.Capture]:
        """Good captures after the cut-off whose bytes differ from the chosen one's."""
        if not source.original:
            return []
        every = wayback.captures(self.client, source.original)
        taken = next((c for c in every if wayback.id_url(c.timestamp, source.original) == chosen), None)
        after = wayback.select(every, wayback.Pick(after=source.pick.before, mimetype=source.pick.mimetype))
        return [after] if after and (not taken or after.digest != taken.digest) else []


def read_manifest(path: Path = MANIFEST) -> list[dict]:
    if not path.is_file():
        return []
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        return list(csv.DictReader(handle))


def write_manifest(rows: list[dict], path: Path = MANIFEST) -> None:
    tmp = path.with_suffix(".csv.tmp")
    with tmp.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=COLUMNS, lineterminator="\n")
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)
    tmp.replace(path)


def row_for(source: Source, url: str, known: dict[str, dict]) -> dict:
    old = known.get(source.path)
    same = old is not None and old["url"] == url
    return {
        "carrier": source.carrier,
        "year": source.year,
        "path": source.path,
        "url": url,
        "method": source.method,
        "sha256": old["sha256"] if same else "",
        "bytes": old["bytes"] if same else "",
        "in_git": "true" if source.in_git else "false",
        "content": source.content,
    }


def discover(client: wayback.CdxClient, sources: list[Source], known: dict[str, dict],
             log=print) -> tuple[list[dict], list[str]]:
    """Manifest rows for `sources`, and the problems that kept some out."""
    d = Discovery(client)
    rows, problems = [], []
    for i, source in enumerate(sources, 1):
        try:
            url = d.url(source)
        except (Unresolved, OSError) as error:
            problems.append(f"{source.path}: {error}")
            log(f"!! {source.path}: {error}")
            continue
        log(f"{i:3d}/{len(sources)} {source.path}")
        rows.append(row_for(source, url, known))
    return rows, problems


def differences(new: list[dict], old: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    out = []
    old_by = {r["path"]: r for r in old}
    new_paths = {r["path"] for r in new}
    for r in new:
        before = old_by.get(r["path"])
        if before is None:
            out.append(f"+ {r['path']}  {r['url']}")
            continue
        for column in ("url", "method", "in_git", "content", "carrier", "year"):
            if r[column] != before[column]:
                out.append(f"~ {r['path']}  {column}: {before[column]!r} -> {r[column]!r}")
    out += [f"- {r['path']}" for r in old if r["path"] not in new_paths]
    if not out and [r["path"] for r in new] != [r["path"] for r in old]:
        out.append("~ row order differs")
    return out


def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.split("\n")[0])
    parser.add_argument("--write", action="store_true", help="rewrite data/raw/manifest.csv")
    parser.add_argument("--newer", action="store_true", help="also report captures after each cut-off")
    parser.add_argument("--carrier", help="only this carrier (with --write, other rows are kept as they are)")
    parser.add_argument("--offline", type=Path, nargs="?", const=FIXTURE, help="answer CDX queries from a recording")
    parser.add_argument("--record", type=Path, nargs="?", const=FIXTURE, help="save every CDX response to a recording")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    client = wayback.RecordedCdx(args.offline) if args.offline else wayback.LiveCdx(keep=bool(args.record))
    old = read_manifest()
    known = {r["path"]: r for r in old}
    sources = [s for s in SOURCES if not args.carrier or s.carrier == args.carrier]
    rows, problems = discover(client, sources, known)

    if args.carrier:
        mine = {r["path"]: r for r in rows}
        rows = [mine.get(r["path"], r) for r in old if r["carrier"] != args.carrier or r["path"] in mine]
        rows += [r for r in mine.values() if r["path"] not in known]
    changes = differences(rows, old)
    print(f"\n{len(rows)} rows, {len(changes)} differences from the manifest, {len(problems)} unresolved")
    for line in changes:
        print("  " + line)

    if args.newer:
        d = Discovery(client)
        by_path = {r["path"]: r for r in rows}
        for source in sources:
            if source.path in by_path:
                for capture in d.newer(source, by_path[source.path]["url"]):
                    print(f"  newer: {source.path}  {capture.timestamp} ({capture.mimetype})")
    if args.record:
        client.save(args.record)
        print(f"recorded {len(client.kept)} CDX responses to {args.record}")
    if args.write and not problems:
        write_manifest(rows)
        print(f"wrote {MANIFEST}")
    elif args.write:
        print("not writing the manifest: some sources could not be resolved")
    return 1 if problems or (changes and not args.write) else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

```

```
"""Tracked raw files must be present and match their manifest checksum."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib

import pytest

from data.pipeline.common import RAW_DIR, manifest_rows


def sha256_of(path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1 << 20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def test_manifest_columns():
    assert list(manifest_rows()[0].keys()) == [
        "carrier", "year", "path", "url", "method", "sha256", "bytes", "in_git", "content"
    ]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("row", [pytest.param(r, id=r["path"]) for r in manifest_rows() if r["in_git"] == "true"])
def test_tracked_file_matches_checksum(row):
    path = RAW_DIR / row["path"]
    assert path.is_file(), f"{row['path']} tracked in git but missing"
    assert sha256_of(path) == row["sha256"], f"{row['path']} checksum differs from manifest"
    assert str(path.stat().st_size) == row["bytes"]


def test_wayback_urls_use_passthrough():
    bad = [r["path"] for r in manifest_rows() if r["method"] == "wayback" and "id_/" not in r["url"]]
    assert not bad, bad
```
